### [1] [Deductive Verification of Weak Memory Programs with View-based Protocols (extended version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21084)
*Ömer Şakar,Soham Chakraborty,Marieke Huisman,Anton Wijs*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在VerCors自动演绎验证工具中编码弱内存并发的方法，开发了VerCors-relaxed扩展，通过扩展原子操作支持并引入协议自动机概念，将基于权限的分离逻辑（如SLR）应用于弱内存并发模型的自动化验证，并在多个文献案例上实现了实际可行的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱内存并发下的程序验证面临挑战，现有程序逻辑多限于人工手动证明，而自动化验证工具VerCors仅支持顺序一致性模型，缺少对弱内存并发的自动化演绎验证支持。

Method: 提出编码弱内存并发的方法，开发VerCors-relaxed扩展：扩展VerCors的原子操作支持，引入协议自动机概念来编码基于权限的分离逻辑，并将SLR程序逻辑的relaxed片段编码到VerCors-relaxed中。

Result: 在VerCors-relaxed上成功编码了SLR逻辑的relaxed片段，并自动验证了多个文献中的示例程序，达到了实际可行的性能表现。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了弱内存并发程序自动演绎验证的空白，证明了将弱内存并发逻辑编码到现有自动化验证工具中的可行性。

Abstract: Concurrent programming under weak memory concurrency faces substantial challenges to ensure correctness due to program behaviors that cannot be explained by thread interleaving, a.k.a. sequential consistency. While several program logics are proposed to reason about weak memory concurrency, their usage has been limited to intricate manual proofs. On the other hand, the VerCors verifier provides a rich toolset for automated deductive verification for sequential consistency.
  In this paper, we bridge this gap for automated deductive verification of weak memory concurrent programs with the VerCors deductive verification tool. We propose an approach to encode weak memory concurrency in VerCors. We develop VerCors-relaxed, where we extend the VerCors atomics support and bring concepts from several protocol automata to encode permission-based separation logics for weak memory concurrency models. To demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, we encode the relaxed fragment of the SLR program logic, a recent state-of-the-art permission-based separation logic for weak memory concurrency in VerCors-relaxed, our extension of VerCors. We use the SLR encoding on VerCors-relaxed to automatically verify several examples from the literature within realistic performance.

</details>


### [2] [TAPO-Description Logic for Information Behavior: Refined OBoxes, Inference, and Categorical Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21172)
*Takao Inoué*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层TAPO描述逻辑框架，包含静态描述层（TBox/ABox）、过程层（PBox）和预言机敏感层（OBox），并引入元层守卫-判断机制来控制过程分支与迭代，通过范畴论语义和层论精细化为该框架提供了统一的逻辑基础，用于建模信息行为中的静态知识、犹豫、外部咨询和行动引导更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的描述逻辑主要关注静态知识表示，难以统一建模信息行为中的动态过程（如犹豫、外部咨询和行动引导更新）。作者旨在构建一个能够同时处理静态知识和动态信息行为的统一逻辑框架。

Method: 1) 将TAPO描述逻辑重构为分层形式化系统：静态描述层(TBox/ABox)、过程层(PBox)、预言机敏感层(OBox)；2) 引入元层守卫-判断层(guard-judgment layer)控制过程分支与迭代；3) 构建核心推理系统，涵盖静态推理、守卫过程转换和验证的外部导入；4) 给出范畴论语义并指出层论( sheaf-theoretic )精细化方向。

Result: 建立了一个分层TAPO描述逻辑框架及其核心推理系统，并给出了范畴论语义。通过信息搜索行为和咖喱餐厅中基于评价的排序行为等实例，展示了该框架能建模静态知识表示、犹豫、外部咨询和行动引导更新。

Conclusion: 该分层TAPO描述逻辑框架能够在统一的逻辑设定中处理静态知识表示、犹豫、外部咨询和行动引导更新等多种信息行为，为信息行为分析提供了新的逻辑工具。

Abstract: This paper develops a refined version of TAPO-description logic for the analysis of information behavior. The framework is treated not as a single homogeneous object logic, but as a layered formalism consisting of a static descriptive layer (TBox/ABox), a procedural layer (PBox), and an oracle-sensitive layer (OBox). To make this architecture mathematically explicit, we introduce a metalevel guard-judgment layer governing procedural branching and iteration. On this basis we formulate a core inference system for TAPO-description logic, covering static TBox/ABox reasoning, guarded procedural transition in the PBox, and validated external import in the OBox. We then give a categorical semantics for the resulting framework and indicate its sheaf-theoretic refinement. The theory is illustrated by examples of information-seeking behavior, including simple search behavior and review-sensitive ordering behavior in a curry restaurant. The aim is to treat not only static knowledge representation but also hesitation, external consultation, and action-guiding update within a unified logical setting.

</details>


### [3] [Using ASP(Q) to Handle Inconsistent Prioritized Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21603)
*Meghyn Bienvenu,Camille Bourgaux,Robin Jean,Giuseppe Mazzotta*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文利用回答集编程(ASP)及其量化扩展ASP(Q)，针对带优先级的冲突数据提出三种最优修复概念（帕累托最优、全局最优和完成最优），并基于这些最优修复定义了AR、brave和IAR三种语义的变体。论文首次实现了全局最优修复语义和基语义（一种可处理的欠近似），实验评估了不同语义、近似和编码下的查询回答可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在带优先级的冲突数据中，如何定义最优修复并进行不一致容忍查询回答是一个关键问题。现有方法缺乏对全局最优修复语义的实现，且需要探索不同语义和近似在实际中的可行性。

Method: 采用ASP和ASP(Q)建模带优先级的数据，定义帕累托最优、全局最优和完成最优三种最优修复；基于这些修复扩展AR、brave和IAR三种语义；引入基语义作为可处理的欠近似；通过实验比较不同语义和编码的性能。

Result: 对于一大类逻辑理论，查询回答处于多项式层级的第一或第二层。首次实现了全局最优修复语义和基语义。实验评估了不同语义和近似下计算答案的可行性。

Conclusion: ASP和ASP(Q)能有效建模带优先级的冲突数据中的最优修复语义，全局最优修复语义在大类理论中具有可计算的复杂度，且实验证明其实现是可行的。

Abstract: We explore the use of answer set programming (ASP) and its extension with quantifiers, ASP(Q), for inconsistency-tolerant querying of prioritized data, where a priority relation between conflicting facts is exploited to define three notions of optimal repairs (Pareto-, globally- and completion-optimal). We consider the variants of three well-known semantics (AR, brave and IAR) that use these optimal repairs, and for which query answering is in the first or second level of the polynomial hierarchy for a large class of logical theories. Notably, this paper presents the first implementation of globally-optimal repair-based semantics, as well as the first implementation of the grounded semantics, which is a tractable under-approximation of all these optimal repair-based semantics. Our experimental evaluation sheds light on the feasibility of computing answers under globally-optimal repair semantics and the impact of adopting different semantics, approximations, and encodings.

</details>


### [4] [A-IC3: Learning-Guided Adaptive Inductive Generalization for Hardware Model Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21688)
*Xiaofeng Zhou,Guangyu Hu,Hongce Zhang,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于多臂老虎机（MAB）的轻量级机器学习框架，用于动态选择IC3算法中的归纳泛化策略，从而替代传统固定策略，显著提升模型检测性能。在914个基准实例上，该方法比基线多解决26-50个案例，PAR-2分数提升194.72-389.29。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IC3算法中的归纳泛化阶段对性能至关重要，但现有方法使用固定策略，忽略了验证环境动态变化的特性，导致生成的子句质量有限，制约了算法效率。

Method: 提出轻量级机器学习框架，采用多臂老虎机（MAB）算法根据实时反馈动态选择归纳泛化策略，通过评估泛化结果的质量来持续更新智能体的策略选择。

Result: 在包含914个实例（主要来自最新HWMCC数据集）的基准测试上，基于SOTA模型检测器rIC3实现，比基线多解决26-50个案例，PAR-2分数提升194.72-389.29。

Conclusion: 基于MAB的动态策略选择框架有效克服了固定策略的局限性，显著提升了IC3算法的求解能力和效率，验证了机器学习在硬件模型检测中自适应优化的潜力。

Abstract: The IC3 algorithm represents the state-of-the-art (SOTA) hardware model checking technique, owing to its robust performance and scalability. A significant body of research has focused on enhancing the solving efficiency of the IC3 algorithm, with particular attention to the inductive generalization process: a critical phase wherein the algorithm seeks to generalize a counterexample to inductiveness (CTI), which typically is a state leading to a bad state, into a broader set of states. This inductive generalization is a primary source of clauses in IC3 and thus plays a pivotal role in determining the overall effectiveness of the algorithm.
  Despite its importance, existing approaches often rely on fixed inductive generalization strategies, overlooking the dynamic and context-sensitive nature of the verification environment in which spurious counterexamples arise. This rigidity can limit the quality of generated clauses and, consequently, the performance of IC3.
  To address this limitation, we propose a lightweight machine-learning-based framework that dynamically selects appropriate inductive generalization strategies in response to the evolving verification context. Specifically, we employ a multi-armed bandit (MAB) algorithm to adaptively choose inductive generalization strategies based on real-time feedback from the verification process. The agent is updated by evaluating the quality of generalization outcomes, thereby refining its strategy selection over time.
  Empirical evaluation on a benchmark suite comprising 914 instances, primarily drawn from the latest HWMCC collection, demonstrates the efficacy of our approach. When implemented on the state-of-the-art model checker rIC3, our method solves 26 to 50 more cases than the baselines and improves the PAR-2 score by 194.72 to 389.29.

</details>

### [5] [Biomedical systems biology workflow orchestration and execution with PoSyMed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20906)
*Simon Süwer,Zoe Chervontseva,Kester Bagemihl,Jan Baumbach,Olga Tsoy,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PoSyMed是一个开源模块化平台，通过后端架构、容器化执行、形式化工具描述和LLM辅助对话界面，整合生物信息学工具与工作流，提升可重复性、可追溯性和透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物信息学软件快速增长导致工具分布碎片化、文档不一致、依赖复杂、执行环境难以复现，即使是有经验的用户也难以重用已有工具和适配工作流。

Method: PoSyMed采用后端中心架构，结合形式化工具描述、基于容器的受控构建与执行流程、持久化工作流状态、对话式用户界面；LLM作为有边界的语义助手辅助工具识别、工作流步骤建议和参数化，而非自主决策者。

Result: 在代表性生物软件场景中评估了系统的工作流支持、交互设计和平台可扩展性，平台已公开可用。

Conclusion: PoSyMed通过统一平台提升了生物医学分析的实用性、可重复性和透明度，将LLM融入受控的人类监督环境而非作为自主决策者。

Abstract: The rapid growth of scientific software has created practical barriers for bioinformatics research. Although powerful statistical, artificial intelligence (AI)-based methods are now widely available, their effective use is often hindered by fragmented distribution, inconsistent documentation, complex dependencies, and difficult-to-reproduce execution environments. As a result, reusing published tools and workflow adaptation to own date remains technically demanding and time-intensive, even for experienced users. Here, we present PoSyMed, an open and modular platform for the controlled integration, composition, and execution of bioinformatics tools and workflows. PoSyMed combines a backend-centered platform architecture with formal tool descriptions, controlled container-based build and execution processes, persistent workflow state, and a dialogue-based user interface. Large language models (LLM) are integrated not as autonomous decision-makers, but as human-computer interface with bounded semantic assistants that help identify tools, propose workflow steps, and support parameterization within a typed, validated, and human-supervised execution environment. PoSyMed is designed to improve reproducibility, traceability, and transparency in practical biomedical analysis within one platform. We describe the system architecture and evaluate its behavior across representative biological software scenarios with respect to workflow support, interaction design, and platform extensibility. PoSyMed is publicly available at https://apps.cosy.bio/posymed.

</details>


### [6] [Learning Reasoning World Models for Parallel Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20926)
*Gautam Singh,Arjun Guha,Bhavya Kailkhura,Harshitha Menon*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出并行代码世界模型（PCWMs），通过让LLM学习直接预测工具执行结果（如数据竞争和性能特征），替代外部工具调用。通过探索-执行-合成反思轨迹的流程训练模型，7B模型在竞态预测准确率从64.3%提升至72.8%，8B模型在性能分析任务上从49.3%提升至58.6%。在修复数据竞争任务中，世界模型反馈相比自我反馈提升修复率2.7%-11.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在串行代码生成方面表现优异，但并行代码由于训练数据稀缺而表现不佳。现有解决方案使用编码智能体调用外部工具，但工具调用成本高昂且在某些场景（如部分代码）中不适用。

Method: 设计了一个探索与数据生成流水线：1）采样多样化的并行编码问题和候选实现；2）通过工具执行记录数据竞争和性能特征；3）合成事后反思轨迹，将源代码与观察到的工具结果因果关联起来。基于这些数据对LLM进行微调得到PCWM。

Result: 7B模型在竞态预测准确率从64.3%提升至72.8%；8B模型在性能分析任务上从49.3%提升至58.6%。在数据竞争修复任务中，世界模型反馈相比自我反馈，7B模型提升2.7%-9.1%，14B模型提升6.1%-11.1%。

Conclusion: 推理模型有潜力作为并行编码智能体中外部工具调用的实用替代方案，能够在不增加工具调用成本的情况下提升并行代码生成质量。

Abstract: Large language models have shown remarkable ability in serial code generation, but they still struggle with parallel code for which training data is comparatively scarce. A common remedy is to use coding agents that interact with external tools, but tool calls can be costly and sometimes impractical, e.g., for partially written code. We propose Parallel-Code World Models (PCWMs), reasoning LLMs that aim to predict tool outcomes directly from parallel source code. To train PCWMs, we design a novel exploration and data generation pipeline that samples diverse parallel-coding problems and candidate implementations across multiple domains, then executes them via tools to record data races and performance profiles. From these, we synthesize hindsight reasoning traces that causally connect source code to observed tool outcomes. Fine-tuning on the resulting data yields noticeable gains, with a 7B-parameter world model improving from 64.3% to 72.8% accuracy in race-outcome prediction, while an 8B-parameter model improves in a performance profiling task from 49.3% to 58.6% accuracy. Furthermore, when open-weight models were tasked with fixing data races, world-model feedback improved their race-fixing rates relative to self-feedback by 2.7%-9.1% using our 7B-parameter world model and by 6.1%-11.1% using our 14B-parameter world model. Our results suggest that reasoning models have the potential to serve as practical substitutes for external tool calls in parallel-coding agents.

</details>


### [7] [Residual Risk Analysis in Benign Code: How Far Are We? A Multi-Model Semantic and Structural Similarity Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21051)
*Mohammad Farhad,Shuvalaxmi Dass*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Residual Risk Scoring (RRS)框架，通过语义嵌入、AST结构相似性和跨模型一致性评估补丁后的残余安全风险，发现约61%的高风险代码对存在13类残余问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漏洞基准将修补后的函数视为天然安全，忽略了残余安全风险的可能性，亟需一种评估补丁是否完全消除风险的方法。

Method: 利用PrimeVul数据集中的脆弱-良性函数对，结合多种代码语言模型的语义相似性分析和Tree-sitter抽象语法树结构分析，提出RRS统一框架整合三类信号。

Result: 约61%的高RSS代码对存在13类残余问题（如空指针解引用、不安全内存分配），经Cppcheck、Clang-Tidy、Facebook-Infer等工具验证。

Conclusion: 代码级相似性可作为优先检查补丁后风险的实用信号，有助于实现更可靠、可扩展的软件安全评估。

Abstract: Software security relies on effective vulnerability detection and patching, yet determining whether a patch fully eliminates risk remains an underexplored challenge. Existing vulnerability benchmarks often treat patched functions as inherently benign, overlooking the possibility of residual security risks. In this work, we analyze vulnerable-benign function pairs from the PrimeVul, a benchmark dataset using multiple code language models (Code LMs) to capture semantic similarity, complemented by Tree-sitter-based abstract syntax tree (AST) analysis for structural similarity. Building on these signals, we propose Residual Risk Scoring (RRS), a unified framework that integrates embedding-based semantic similarity, localized AST-based structural similarity, and cross-model agreement to estimate residual risk in code. Our analysis shows that benign functions often remain highly similar to their vulnerable counterparts both semantically and structurally, indicating potential persistence of residual risk. We further find that approximately $61\%$ of high-RRS code pairs exhibit $13$ distinct categories of residual issues (e.g., null pointer dereferences, unsafe memory allocation), validated using state-of-the-art static analysis tools including Cppcheck, Clang-Tidy, and Facebook-Infer. These results demonstrate that code-level similarity provides a practical signal for prioritizing post-patch inspection, enabling more reliable and scalable security assessment in real-world open-source software pipelines.

</details>


### [8] [A Ground-Truth-Based Evaluation of Vulnerability Detection Across Multiple Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21111)
*Peter Mandl,Paul Mandl,Martin Häusl,Maximilian Auch*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文基于OSV数据库构建了针对多个软件生态系统的漏洞检测评估数据集，并实证分析了不同漏洞检测工具在该数据集上的表现差异。研究提供了可复现数据集构建的开源工具，强调了透明数据集构建对可重复安全研究的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化漏洞检测工具被广泛使用，但由于漏洞数据源的异构结构、标识符方案不一致以及版本范围规范的歧义，评估这些工具仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 从OSV数据库构建了一个将漏洞映射到具体软件包版本的标注真实数据集，并基于该数据集系统比较了不同漏洞检测工具和服务的结果。同时提供了一个开源工具，用于根据论文所述方法从当前OSV数据库中自动重建该数据集。

Result: 揭示了不同漏洞检测系统之间存在系统性差异，证明了透明的数据集构建方法对可重复实证安全研究的重要性。

Conclusion: 漏洞数据库持续更新，本研究使用的数据集代表评估时的漏洞快照；为支持可重复性和未来研究，提供了可自动重建数据集的开源工具。

Abstract: Automated vulnerability detection tools are widely used to identify security vulnerabilities in software dependencies. However, the evaluation of such tools remains challenging due to the heterogeneous structure of vulnerability data sources, inconsistent identifier schemes, and ambiguities in version range specifications. In this paper, we present an empirical evaluation of vulnerability detection across multiple software ecosystems using a curated ground-truth dataset derived from the Open Source Vulnerabilities (OSV) database. The dataset explicitly maps vulnerabilities to concrete package versions and enables a systematic comparison of detection results across different tools and services. Since vulnerability databases such as OSV are continuously updated, the dataset used in this study represents a snapshot of the vulnerability landscape at the time of the evaluation. To support reproducibility and future studies, we provide an open-source tool that automatically reconstructs the dataset from the current OSV database using the methodology described in this paper. Our evaluation highlights systematic differences between vulnerability detection systems and demonstrates the importance of transparent dataset construction for reproducible empirical security research.

</details>


### [9] [Hidden Dependencies and Component Variants in SBOM-Based Software Composition Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21278)
*Shawn Rasheed,Max McPhee,Lisa Patterson,Stephen MacDonell,Jens Dietrich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了SBOM中两类不匹配模式：隐藏的代码级依赖关系和组件变体（克隆），这些不匹配导致不同SBOM漏洞扫描器之间报告结果不一致以及VEX声明处理不一致，揭示了当前SBOM生产和消费的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着供应链攻击的增加，SBOM成为漏洞管理的重要技术，但其有效性取决于SBOM准确表示组件身份和实际依赖关系的程度。现有研究对SBOM中隐藏的代码级依赖关系和组件变体克隆问题关注不足，这些问题可能导致漏洞扫描结果不一致。

Method: 通过实证研究识别两类不匹配模式：1）隐藏的代码级依赖关系（未被表示为组件级依赖关系）；2）组件变体/克隆（扫描器无法一致识别）。通过比较不同SBOM漏洞扫描器在不匹配场景下的漏洞报告和VEX声明处理结果。

Result: 发现这些不匹配模式会导致：1）不同SBOM漏洞扫描器之间的漏洞报告不一致；2）VEX声明处理结果不一致。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了当前SBOM生产和消费的局限性，表明需要更丰富的依赖表示和组件身份标识机制来提升SBOM的准确性和有效性。

Abstract: Software Bills of Material (SBOMs) have emerged as an important technology for vulnerability management amid rising supply-chain attacks. They represent component relationships within a software product and support software composition analysis (SCA) by linking components to known vulnerabilities. However, the effectiveness of SBOM-based analysis depends on how accurately SBOMs represent component identities and actual dependencies in software. This paper studies two mismatch patterns: hidden code-level dependencies that are not represented as component-level dependencies, and component variants (clones) that cannot be identified consistently by scanners. We show that these mismatches can lead to inconsistent vulnerability reporting and inconsistent handling of VEX statements across popular SBOM-based vulnerability scanners. These results highlight limitations in current SBOM production and consumption and motivate richer dependency representation and component identity.

</details>


### [10] [Conjecture and Inquiry: Quantifying Software Performance Requirements via Interactive Retrieval-Augmented Preference Elicitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21380)
*Wang Shi Hai,Chen Tao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了IRAP方法，通过交互式检索增强的偏好引导，将自然语言描述的性能需求自动量化为数学函数，实验表明在少至5轮交互下性能提升高达40倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件性能需求以自然语言描述，存在模糊性和认知不确定性，导致自动化量化成为一个未解决且具有挑战性的问题。

Method: 提出IRAP框架，通过交互式检索增强的偏好引导（retrieval-augmented preference elicitation），从问题特定知识中检索和推理偏好，并指导与利益相关者的渐进式交互，同时降低认知负担。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上与10种最先进方法对比，IRAP在所有案例中均表现优越，仅需少至5轮交互即可实现高达40倍的性能提升。

Conclusion: IRAP有效解决了性能需求自动化量化的模糊性问题，通过检索增强的交互式偏好引导实现了显著优于现有方法的效果。

Abstract: Since software performance requirements are documented in natural language, quantifying them into mathematical forms is essential for software engineering. Yet, the vagueness in performance requirements and uncertainty of human cognition have caused highly uncertain ambiguity in the interpretations, rendering their automated quantification an unaddressed and challenging problem. In this paper, we formalize the problem and propose IRAP, an approach that quantifies performance requirements into mathematical functions via interactive retrieval-augmented preference elicitation. IRAP differs from the others in that it explicitly derives from problem-specific knowledge to retrieve and reason the preferences, which also guides the progressive interaction with stakeholders, while reducing the cognitive overhead. Experiment results against 10 state-of-the-art methods on four real-world datasets demonstrate the superiority of IRAP on all cases with up to 40x improvements under as few as five rounds of interactions.

</details>


### [11] [Assessing the Impact of Requirement Ambiguity on LLM-based Function-Level Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21505)
*Di Yang,Xinou Xie,Xiuwen Yang,Ming Hu,Yihao Huang,Yueling Zhang,Weikai Miao,Ting Su,Chengcheng Wan,Geguang Pu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出了Orchid基准，这是首个专门针对模糊需求设计的代码生成基准，包含1,304个函数级任务，涵盖词汇、句法、语义和歧义四种模糊类型。实验发现，需求模糊性会持续降低所有LLM的代码生成性能，且越先进的模型受影响越大，LLM还缺乏自主识别和解决模糊性的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常假设需求清晰明确，忽视了真实软件开发中自然语言固有的模糊性及其对自动化代码生成的阻碍，缺乏对LLM在模糊需求下行为的系统性理解。

Method: 构建了首个针对模糊需求的代码生成基准Orchid，包含1,304个函数级任务，涵盖四种模糊类型（词汇、句法、语义、歧义）；基于该基准对多种LLM进行系统性实证研究，评估需求模糊性对代码生成的影响。

Result: 模糊性持续降低所有被评估LLM的性能，越先进的模型负面效应越显著；LLM常为同一模糊需求生成功能不同的实现，且缺乏自主识别和解决模糊性的能力。

Conclusion: 清晰需求与模糊需求之间存在显著的性能差距，亟需在下一代自动化软件工程工具中引入模糊感知技术。

Abstract: Software requirement ambiguity is ubiquitous in real-world development, stemming from the inherent imprecision of natural language and the varying interpretations of stakeholders. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities in generating code from precise specifications, such ambiguity poses a significant obstacle to reliable automated code generation. Existing benchmarks typically assume clear and unambiguous requirements, leaving an empirical gap in understanding how LLMs behave when faced with the inherent uncertainty of real-world software requirements. In this paper, we introduce Orchid, the first code generation benchmark specifically designed with ambiguous requirements. It comprises 1,304 function-level tasks covering four distinct types of ambiguity: lexical, syntactic, semantic, and vagueness. Leveraging this dataset, we conduct the first systematic empirical study to evaluate the impact of requirement ambiguity on LLM-based code generation. Our results demonstrate that ambiguity consistently degrades the performance of all evaluated LLMs, with the most pronounced negative effects observed in highly advanced models. Furthermore, we observe that LLMs frequently produce functionally divergent implementations for the same ambiguous requirement and lack the capability to identify or resolve such ambiguity autonomously. These findings reveal a significant performance gap between clear and ambiguous requirements, underscoring the urgent need for ambiguity-aware techniques in the next generation of automated software engineering tools. The Orchid benchmark is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/SII-YDD/Orchid.

</details>


### [12] [SpecSyn: LLM-based Synthesis and Refinement of Formal Specifications for Real-world Program Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21570)
*Lezhi Ma,Shangqing Liu,Yi Li,Qiong Wu,Han Wang,Lei Bu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出SpecSyn，一种基于LLM的规约自动生成方法，通过程序分解和基于语义变异区分的规约精炼机制，在高精度(>90%)和高召回率(>75%)下显著优于现有方法，并成功处理了真实程序中1071/1365个目标属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序验证需要构建完整的跨过程规约，但人工构建耗时耗力。现有LLM方法缺乏处理大规模输入程序的有效策略，也没有评估和保证生成规约质量的机制，难以从真实复杂程序中提取精确规约来支持目标属性的验证。

Method: SpecSyn首先将输入程序分解为独立的片段，分别由后续的迭代规约生成过程处理。创新性地引入基于语义非等价程序变异和变异区分的规约精炼机制，评估并增强生成规约的语义强度。

Result: SpecSyn保持了超过90%的高精度和超过75%的优异召回率，显著优于现有基于LLM的方法。在进一步评估中，成功处理了1365个目标属性中的1071个，证明了其在真实世界程序验证任务中的适用性。

Conclusion: SpecSyn通过程序分解和基于语义变异区分的规约精炼机制，有效解决了现有LLM方法在大规模程序和规约质量评估方面的不足，为真实世界程序的自动规约生成和验证提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Program verification is a formal technique to rigorously ensure the correctness and fault-freeness of software systems. However, constructing comprehensive interprocedural specifications for full verification obligations is time-consuming and labor-intensive, giving rise to automated specification generation approaches. Despite the significant advancements in these approaches brought by Large Language Models (LLMs), existing LLM-empowered approaches still suffer from significant limitations: they lack effective strategies for handling sizable input programs, and are typically equipped with no mechanisms to evaluate and guarantee the strength of the generated specifications. The limitations impair their ability to extract precise specifications from real-world complicated programs to support the verification of target properties, thereby hindering the applicability of existing approaches in verification tasks on real-world programs. To remedy this gap, we propose SpecSyn, a novel LLM-based specification generation method. SpecSyn first decomposes the input program into individual segments, which are handled respectively by the subsequent iterative specification generation process. Innovatively, we incorporate into the process a specification refinement mechanism based on semantic-non-equivalent program mutations and variant discrimination, assessing and enhancing the semantic strength of the generated specifications. Extensive experiments show that SpecSyn maintains high precision over 90% and outstanding recall over 75%, significantly outperforming existing LLM-based approaches. In further evaluations, SpecSyn successfully handles 1071 out of 1365 target properties for open-source programs, proving its applicability on real-world program verification tasks.

</details>


### [13] [A Metamorphic Testing Approach to Diagnosing Memorization in LLM-Based Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21579)
*Milan De Koning,Ali Asgari,Pouria Derakhshanfar,Annibale Panichella*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过将蜕变测试与负对数似然（NLL）相结合，揭示了LLM在自动程序修复评估中存在数据泄漏问题。在Defects4J和GitBug-Java基准测试上，七种先进LLM在变换后的基准测试中补丁生成成功率大幅下降（-4.1%至-15.98%），且下降幅度与NLL强相关，表明模型在可能记忆过的实例上表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在自动程序修复任务中表现优异，但先前评估结果可能因数据泄漏（模型预训练数据与评估基准重叠）而被高估。需要更可靠的方法来检测和缓解这种数据泄漏。

Method: 将蜕变测试（MT）与负对数似然（NLL）相结合。通过对Defects4J和GitBug-Java数据集应用语义保持变换构建变体基准，评估七种LLM在原始和变换版本上的修复成功率，并分析性能下降与NLL之间的相关性。

Result: 所有评估的LLM在变换基准上补丁生成成功率均显著下降（GPT-4o下降-4.1%，Llama-3.1下降-15.98%），且性能下降与原始基准上的NLL强相关。

Conclusion: MT与NLL结合能提供更强、更可靠的数据泄漏证据；单独使用蜕变测试也可帮助缓解LLM-based APR评估中的数据泄漏影响。

Abstract: LLM-based automated program repair (APR) techniques have shown promising results in reducing debugging costs. However, prior results can be affected by data leakage: large language models (LLMs) may memorize bug fixes when evaluation benchmarks overlap with their pretraining data, leading to inflated performance estimates. In this paper, we investigate whether we can better reveal data leakage by combining metamorphic testing (MT) with negative log-likelihood (NLL), which has been used in prior work as a proxy for memorization. We construct variant benchmarks by applying semantics-preserving transformations to two widely used datasets, Defects4J and GitBug-Java. Using these benchmarks, we evaluate the repair success rates of seven LLMs on both original and transformed versions, and analyze the relationship between performance degradation and NLL. Our results show that all evaluated state-of-the-art LLMs exhibit substantial drops in patch generation success rates on transformed benchmarks, ranging from -4.1% for GPT-4o to -15.98% for Llama-3.1. Furthermore, we find that this degradation strongly correlates with NLL on the original benchmarks, suggesting that models perform better on instances they are more likely to have memorized. These findings show that combining MT with NLL provides stronger and more reliable evidence of data leakage, while metamorphic testing alone can help mitigate its effects in LLM-based APR evaluations.

</details>


### [14] [DryRUN: On the Role of Public Tests in LLM-Driven Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21598)
*Kaushitha Silva,Srinath Perera*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了DryRUN框架，该框架无需人工提供的公共测试用例，通过让大模型自主生成输入并模拟执行轨迹来自我修正代码，消除了对ground-truth样本的依赖，在LiveCodeBench v6数据集上达到了与依赖公共测试用例的SOTA方法CodeSIM相当的性能，同时降低了输出token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模拟驱动规划与调试的多智能体代码生成框架依赖人工提供的公共测试用例来引导调试和模拟循环，而人工编写全面的输入输出示例是软件开发生命周期中的劳动密集型瓶颈；并且依赖公共测试会导致"过度自信差距"，使框架过度拟合简单示例而在隐藏评估中失败。

Method: 提出DryRUN框架，核心思想是大语言模型可以自主生成有效输入并模拟执行轨迹进行自我修正。该框架无需ground-truth样本，通过让LLM迭代规划、自主生成输入并模拟执行来缓解算法过拟合。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench v6数据集（2025年3月后）上，DryRUN与依赖公共测试用例的SOTA方法CodeSIM性能相当，且完全无需公共测试用例或外部执行反馈，同时减少了输出token消耗。

Conclusion: 外部样本输入对于代码生成并非严格必要，大模型可以自主生成有效输入并模拟执行轨迹来自我修正。DryRUN消除了代码生成框架对人工标注测试用例的依赖，为实际软件开发场景提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent frameworks are widely used in autonomous code generation and have applications in complex algorithmic problem-solving. Recent work has addressed the challenge of generating functionally correct code by incorporating simulation-driven planning and debugging, where language models trace execution steps to verify logic. However, these approaches depend on human-provided public test cases to ground the debugging and simulation loop. Manually authoring comprehensive input-output examples is a labor-intensive bottleneck in the software development lifecycle. Because ground-truth input-output examples are rarely available prior to implementation in real-world software engineering, this dependency restricts methods to curated competitive programming benchmarks. Furthermore, we identify that reliance on these public tests induces an ``overconfidence gap,'' causing frameworks to overfit to simplistic examples and fail on hidden evaluations. In contrast, we observe that external sample inputs are not strictly necessary for code generation. We demonstrate that large language models can autonomously generate valid inputs and simulate execution traces to self-correct. Consequently, we develop DryRUN, a framework that eliminates the need for ground-truth samples by allowing the LLM to iteratively plan, autonomously generate its own inputs and simulate execution, mitigating algorithmic overconfidence. Evaluations on the LiveCodeBench v6 dataset (post-March 2025) demonstrate that DryRUN matches performance against CodeSIM, a state-of-the-art and public-test-dependent framework, while operating entirely without public test cases or external execution feedback while reducing output token consumption.

</details>


### [15] [Verifying Machine Learning Interpretability Requirements through Provenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21599)
*Lynn Vonderhaar,Juan Couder,Daryela Cisneros,Omar Ochoa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出利用ML provenance（机器学习的来源/溯源数据）来验证可解释性这一非功能需求，通过将不可度量的可解释性需求转化为可量化的功能需求，从而实现对其的验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献虽然定义了ML领域的可解释性需求，但可解释性作为一个不可度量的非功能需求（NFR），无法被明确验证。作者希望解决这一验证难题。

Method: 提出通过保存模型和数据 provenance（溯源信息），使模型行为透明化和可解释化，将这些溯源数据转化为可量化的功能需求（FRs），通过对这些功能需求的验证来间接验证可解释性NFR。

Result: 提供了一套方法，让ML工程师能够保存多种类型的模型和数据溯源信息，以此建立可量化的功能需求，从而实现对可解释性非功能需求的验证。

Conclusion: 本文贡献了一种验证机器学习模型可解释性非功能需求的方法，填补了ML工程中可解释性需求无法验证的空白。

Abstract: Machine Learning (ML) Engineering is a growing field that necessitates an increase in the rigor of ML development. It draws many ideas from software engineering and more specifically, from requirements engineering. Existing literature on ML Engineering defines quality models and Non-Functional Requirements (NFRs) specific to ML, in particular interpretability being one such NFR. However, a major challenge occurs in verifying ML NFRs, including interpretability. Although existing literature defines interpretability in terms of ML, it remains an immeasurable requirement, making it impossible to definitively confirm whether a model meets its interpretability requirement. This paper shows how ML provenance can be used to verify ML interpretability requirements. This work provides an approach for how ML engineers can save various types of model and data provenance to make the model's behavior transparent and interpretable. Saving this data forms the basis of quantifiable Functional Requirements (FRs) whose verification in turn verifies the interpretability NFR. Ultimately, this paper contributes a method to verify interpretability NFRs for ML models.

</details>


### [16] [Can Large Language Models Assist the Comprehension of ROS2 Software Architectures?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21699)
*Laura Duits,Bouazza El Moutaouakil,Ivano Malavolta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过1,230个提示、9个LLM和3个ROS2系统的受控实验，评估LLM在ROS2架构理解中的表现。平均准确率达98.22%，gemini-2.5-pro表现最佳（100%准确率），但最复杂系统贡献了249个错误回答中的大部分。解释的连贯性分数在0.394到0.762之间，困惑度差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ROS2是机器人软件开发的主流框架，其架构高度复杂、组件众多且通信去中心化。研究者希望评估LLM能否帮助开发者理解ROS2系统的架构信息，从而降低理解门槛、提升开发效率。

Method: 设计受控实验：提供可生成ROS2系统架构相关问题的通用算法，针对3个规模递增的ROS2系统，向9个LLM共提交1,230个问题。以实际运行和监控3个ROS2系统建立基准真相，评估LLM回答的准确性，并定性分析其解释质量。

Result: 所有LLM的平均准确率为98.22%。gemini-2.5-pro达100%，o3为99.77%，gemini-2.5-flash为99.72%，最差的gpt-4.1为95%。1,230个提示中仅300个回答错误，其中249个涉及最复杂系统。解释连贯性得分范围为0.394（"服务引用"）到0.762（"通信路径"）。平均困惑度从chatgpt-4o的19.6到o4-mini的103.6不等。

Conclusion: LLM在辅助ROS2开发者理解软件架构方面潜力巨大，但开发者需意识到不同LLM的内在局限性和性能差异，在使用时加以考量。

Abstract: Context. The most used development framework for robotics software is ROS2. ROS2 architectures are highly complex, with thousands of components communicating in a decentralized fashion. Goal. We aim to evaluate how LLMs can assist in the comprehension of factual information about the architecture of ROS2 systems. Method. We conduct a controlled experiment where we administer 1,230 prompts to 9 LLMs containing architecturally-relevant questions about 3 ROS2 systems with incremental size. We provide a generic algorithm that systematically generates architecturally-relevant questions for a ROS2 system. Then, we (i) assess the accuracy of the answers of the LLMs against a ground truth established via running and monitoring the 3 ROS2 systems and (ii) qualitatively analyse the explanations provided by the LLMs. Results. Almost all questions are answered correctly across all LLMs (mean=98.22%). gemini-2.5-pro performs best (100% accuracy across all prompts and systems), followed by o3 (99.77%), and gemini-2.5-flash (99.72%); the least performing LLM is gpt-4.1 (95%). Only 300/1,230 prompts are incorrectly answered, of which 249 are about the most complex system. The coherence scores in LLM's explanations range from 0.394 for "service references" to 0.762 for "communication path". The mean perplexity varies significantly across models, with chatgpt-4o achieving the lowest score (19.6) and o4-mini the highest (103.6). Conclusions. There is great potential in the usage of LLMs to aid ROS2 developers in comprehending non-trivial aspects of the software architecture of their systems. Nevertheless, developers should be aware of the intrinsic limitations and different performances of the LLMs and take those into account when using them.

</details>


### [17] [Automated LTL Specification Generation from Industrial Aerospace Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21715)
*Zhi Ma,Xiao Liang,Cheng Wen,Rui Chen,Bin Gu,Shengchao Qin,Cong Tian,Mengfei Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AeroReq2LTL是一个利用大语言模型将航空领域自然语言需求自动转化为线性时序逻辑(LTL)属性的框架，通过数据字典和模板化需求语言两项工业创新，在实际航空数据集上实现了85%精确率和88%召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空安全关键软件中，将自然语言需求翻译为LTL属性是劳动密集且易出错的过程，需要同时具备航空航天控制工程和形式化方法的稀缺专业知识；现有的NL-to-LTL工具在实际工业需求文档上常因领域术语复杂或隐式时序逻辑结构而失败。

Method: 提出AeroReq2LTL框架，包含两项关键创新：(i) 数据字典——将技术行话规范化为精确的原子命题；(ii) 基于模板的需求语言——在翻译前使时序线索和逻辑关系显式化。利用大语言模型(LLM)进行自动化LTL属性生成。

Result: 在真实航空数据集上，LTL生成达到85%精确率和88%召回率，生成的输出可直接被现有验证工具使用。

Conclusion: AeroReq2LTL有效弥合了工业实践中自然语言需求与形式化验证之间的鸿沟，为航空安全关键软件的自动化验证提供了可行的工业化解决方案。

Abstract: In the development and verification of safety-critical aero-space software, Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) has been widely used to specify complex system properties derived from requirements. However, a significant gap remains in industrial practice: translating natural language (NL) requirements into formal LTL properties is a labor-intensive and error-prone process that requires rare expertise in both aerospace control engineering and formal methods. While recent NL-to-LTL tools (e.g., NL2SPEC, NL2TL, NL2LTL) are capable of automating parts of this process, they often fail on real requirement documents in industrial settings, due to complex domain terminology or implicit temporal and logical structure. To address these challenges, we present AeroReq2LTL, a framework that automates LTL property generation for aerospace requirements using large language models (LLMs), with two key industrial innovations: (i) a data dictionary that normalizes technical jargon into precise atomic propositions; and (ii) a template-based requirement language that makes temporal cues and logical relations explicit before translation. On a real aerospace dataset, AeroReq2LTL achieves 85% precision and 88% recall in LTL generation, and its outputs can be directly consumed by existing verification tools.

</details>


### [18] [Agentic AI-assisted coding offers a unique opportunity to instill epistemic grounding during software development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21744)
*Magnus Palmblad,Jared M. Ragland,Benjamin A. Neely*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出GROUNDING$.$md，一种社区治理的领域范围认知基础文档，以质谱蛋白质组学为例，通过编码硬约束（科学正确性必需的不可协商有效性不变量）和约定参数（社区同意的默认值）来覆盖所有其他上下文，确保AI辅助编码工具生成的软件具有领域最佳实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI辅助编程从聊天式编程演进为完全自主的智能体软件开发，人类开发者制定计划后由智能体AI执行。当前趋势是使用超出计划文档范围的其他文档（如项目级和方法级文档）。然而，这些文档缺乏领域范围的认知基础，导致非领域专家生成的代码可能违反科学正确性的核心要求。因此，需要一种显式的、社区治理的领域范围认知基础文档来确保AI生成软件的科学有效性。

Method: GROUNDING$.$md作为一种社区治理的、领域范围的认知基础文档，编码两类约束：硬约束（Hard Constraints）——科学正确性必需的不可协商有效性不变量；约定参数（Convention Parameters）——社区同意的默认值。这些约束覆盖所有其他上下文，不受用户提示词影响，以确保科学有效性。

Result: 该方法将赋能非领域专家生成从底层就内嵌最佳实践的代码、工具和软件，为软件开发者以及审查或使用最终产品的人提供信心。相比人类，智能体AI更容易遵守指南规范。

Conclusion: 这一机会允许组织以让领域专家保持在循环中的方式开发认知基础文档，在民主化生成定制软件解决方案的未来中，保持领域专家的参与。

Abstract: The capabilities of AI-assisted coding are progressing at breakneck speed. Chat-based vibe coding has evolved into fully fledged AI-assisted, agentic software development using agent scaffolds where the human developer creates a plan that agentic AIs implement. One current trend is utilizing documents beyond this plan document, such as project and method-scoped documents. Here we propose GROUNDING$.$md, a community-governed, field-scoped epistemic grounding document, using mass spectrometry-based proteomics as an example. This explicit field-scoped grounding document encodes Hard Constraints (non-negotiable validity invariants empirically required for scientific correctness) and Convention Parameters (community-agreed defaults) that override all other contexts to enforce validity, regardless of what the user prompts. In practice, this will empower a non-domain expert to generate code, tools, and software that have best practices baked in at the ground level, providing confidence to the software developer but also to those reviewing or using the final product. Undoubtedly it is easier to have agentic AIs adhere to guidelines than humans, and this opportunity allows for organizations to develop epistemic grounding documents in such a way as to keep domain experts in the loop in a future of democratized generation of bespoke software solutions.

</details>


### [19] [Generalizing Test Cases for Comprehensive Test Scenario Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21771)
*Binhang Qi,Yun Lin,Xinyi Weng,Chenyan Liu,Hailong Sun,Gordon Fraser,Jin Song Dong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出TestGeneralizer框架，从单个开发者编写的测试用例出发，通过需求理解、场景模板生成与实例化、测试用例生成与优化三阶段，自动泛化出覆盖多测试场景的完整测试用例集。在12个Java开源项目上，相比ChatTester在变异和LLM评估的场景覆盖率上分别提升31.66%和23.08%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者编写的测试用例往往不全，重要测试常延迟添加甚至直到Bug触发后才补全；现有自动化测试生成技术主要关注代码覆盖率，而实际测试场景源于需求（通常未文档化），与代码控制流分支并不一致。

Method: 三个阶段：1) 增强对被测方法及初始测试背后需求与场景的理解；2) 生成测试场景模板并实例化为多种具体场景；3) 从场景实例生成并优化可执行的测试用例。

Result: 在12个开源Java项目上，相比ChatTester，变异场景覆盖率提升31.66%，LLM评估的场景覆盖率提升23.08%。

Conclusion: 从单个开发者测试出发泛化出覆盖多场景的测试是可行且有效的，弥补了现有代码覆盖导向方法在场景覆盖方面的不足。

Abstract: Test cases are essential for software development and maintenance. In practice, developers derive multiple test cases from an implicit pattern based on their understanding of requirements and inference of diverse test scenarios, each validating a specific behavior of the focal method. However, producing comprehensive tests is time-consuming and error-prone: many important tests that should have accompanied the initial test are added only after a significant delay, sometimes only after bugs are triggered. Existing automated test generation techniques largely focus on code coverage. Yet in real projects, practical tests are seldom driven by code coverage alone, since test scenarios do not necessarily align with control-flow branches. Instead, test scenarios originate from requirements, which are often undocumented and implicitly embedded in a project's design and implementation. However, developer-written tests are frequently treated as executable specifications; thus, even a single initial test that reflects the developer's intent can reveal the underlying requirement and the diverse scenarios that should be validated. In this work, we propose TestGeneralizer, a framework for generalizing test cases to comprehensively cover test scenarios. TestGeneralizer orchestrates three stages: (1) enhancing the understanding of the requirement and scenario behind the focal method and initial test; (2) generating a test scenario template and crystallizing it into various test scenario instances; and (3) generating and refining executable test cases from these instances. We evaluate TestGeneralizer against three state-of-the-art baselines on 12 open-source Java projects. TestGeneralizer achieves significant improvements: +31.66% and +23.08% over ChatTester, in mutation-based and LLM-assessed scenario coverage, respectively.

</details>

### [20] [A Brief History of Fréchet Distances: From Curves and Probability Laws to FID](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21745)
*Yuli Wu*

Main category: cs.GL

TL;DR: 本文追溯了Fréchet距离的发展历程：从1906年Fréchet在抽象集距离方面的博士论文，到1990年代折线曲线间Frêchet距离的算法计算，再到1957年基于耦合的概率分布距离（与Wasserstein距离和最优输运相关），并连接了概率分布上的耦合面与曲线上的几何面，最后以深度生成模型评估中的Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) 作为现代应用案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统梳理Frêchet距离从抽象数学概念到现代机器学习应用的历史脉络，并试图建立概率分布上的耦合面与曲线上的几何面之间的内在联系。

Method: 采用编年史叙述方式，追溯Frêchet距离在三个关键阶段的发展：1906年抽象集距离、1957年概率分布距离（耦合视角）、1990年代折线曲线距离的算法计算，并通过附录提供原始文献的英译。

Result: 揭示了Frêchet距离在几何（曲线）和概率分布（耦合）两个看似不同领域之间的深层联系，并展示了其现代应用FID作为Wasserstein-2距离在多元高斯分布间的具体实例。

Conclusion: Frêchet距离是一个横跨抽象数学、计算几何和现代机器学习的统一概念，其分布耦合面与曲线几何面之间存在可建立的联系，FID是其理论在深度生成模型评估中的成功应用。

Abstract: This note provides a chronological account of Fréchet distances, starting with Maurice Fréchet's 1906 doctoral thesis on distances in abstract sets and tracing the Fréchet distance between polygonal curves and its algorithmic computation in the 1990s. It then continues with his 1957 paper on a coupling-based distance between probability laws with a brief glimpse of Wasserstein distance and optimal transport. We further attempt to draw connections between the distributional, coupling-based facet of Fréchet distances on probability laws and the geometric facet on curves. The note ends with a modern use case, the Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) in the era of deep generative model evaluation, interpretable as the Wasserstein-2 distance between multivariate Gaussians in a learned feature space. An appendix includes \TeX{}ified faithful English translations of Fréchet's 1906 thesis and 1957 paper, and Lévy's 1950 note for reader convenience.

</details>

### [21] [Linear Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21467)
*Arnaud Spiwack,Csongor Kiss,Jean-Philippe Bernardy,Nicolas Wu,Richard A. Eisenberg*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出了线性约束（Linear Constraints）作为Haskell类型类约束的线性对应物。线性约束充当隐式线性参数，由编译器自动填充，消除了显式线性参数带来的繁琐。该特性以限定类型系统（qualified type system）的形式呈现，并扩展了GHC现有的约束求解器算法。其正确性通过脱糖（desugar）到Linear Haskell得到保证。本文是对作者先前工作的修订和扩展，简化了形式系统和约束求解器，并增加了更多应用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Haskell中使用线性类型系统可以安全地管理文件句柄、手动管理的内存等资源，但显式线性参数会导致代码编写变得繁琐（bureaucracy）。线性约束旨在解决这一缺陷，通过编译器自动填充隐式线性参数，减少程序员负担。

Method: 将线性约束设计为限定类型系统（qualified type system），并扩展GHC现有的约束求解器算法来实现类型推断。通过脱糖（desugar）到Linear Haskell来保证线性约束的可靠性和正确性。

Result: 实现了线性约束的隐式参数自动填充机制，简化了线性Haskell代码的编写。形式系统和约束求解器相比先前工作得到显著简化，并展示了更多应用场景。

Conclusion: 线性约束作为Linear Haskell的补充特性，有效降低了线性类型编程的复杂性，同时通过脱糖机制保证了类型安全。简化的形式系统和求解器使其更适合在实际编译器（GHC）中实现和部署。

Abstract: Linear constraints are the linear counterpart of Haskell's class constraints. Linearly typed parameters allow the programmer to control resources such as file handles and manually managed memory as linear arguments. Indeed, a linear type system can verify that these resources are used safely. However, writing code with explicit linear arguments requires bureaucracy. Linear constraints address this shortcoming: a linear constraint acts as an implicit linear argument that can be filled in automatically by the compiler.
  We present this new feature as a qualified type system, together with an inference algorithm which extends GHC's existing constraint solver algorithm. Soundness of linear constraints is ensured by the fact that they desugar into Linear Haskell.
  This paper is a revised and extended version of a previous paper by the same authors (arXiv:2103.06127). The formal system and the constraint solver have been significantly simplified and numerous additional applications are described.

</details>

### [22] [SQLyzr: A Comprehensive Benchmark and Evaluation Platform for Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21214)
*Sepideh Abedini,M. Tamer Özsu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: SQLyzr 是一个面向文本到 SQL 模型的综合评估平台，通过多样化指标、真实工作负载对齐、细粒度查询分类与错误分析，弥补了现有基准测试仅依赖单一聚合分数、缺乏真实场景评估的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到 SQL 基准测试存在三大局限：(1) 仅使用单一聚合分数进行评价，缺乏对生成查询多维度特性的刻画；(2) 未在真实场景下进行评估，缺少与实际 SQL 使用模式和工作负载的对齐；(3) 无法提供不同查询类型下模型行为的细粒度洞察，不利于诊断和迭代改进模型。

Method: SQLyzr 提出了一套综合评估方法，包括：(1) 引入多样化评估指标以捕获生成查询的多个方面；(2) 通过与真实世界 SQL 使用模式对齐以及数据库规模扩展来实现更真实的评估；(3) 支持细粒度查询分类、错误分析和工作负载增强，帮助用户诊断和提升模型性能；同时提供图形化交互界面支持自定义评估设置和细粒度报告分析。

Result: SQLyzr 作为一个完整的基准测试与评估平台，能够提供多样化的评估指标、真实场景对齐、细粒度分类与错误分析等功能，并通过交互式图形界面展示这些能力。源代码已开源。

Conclusion: SQLyzr 通过解决现有基准测试的关键局限性，有望推动文本到 SQL 模型的评估与迭代改进。其开源特性便于社区使用和扩展。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL models have significantly improved with the adoption of Large Language Models (LLMs), leading to their increasing use in real-world applications. Although many benchmarks exist for evaluating the performance of text-to-SQL models, they often rely on a single aggregate score, lack evaluation under realistic settings, and provide limited insight into model behaviour across different query types. In this work, we present SQLyzr, a comprehensive benchmark and evaluation platform for text-to-SQL models. SQLyzr incorporates a diverse set of evaluation metrics that capture multiple aspects of generated queries, while enabling more realistic evaluation through workload alignment with real-world SQL usage patterns and database scaling. It further supports fine-grained query classification, error analysis, and workload augmentation, allowing users to better diagnose and improve text-to-SQL models. This demonstration showcases these capabilities through an interactive experience. Through SQLyzr's graphical interface, users can customize evaluation settings, analyze fine-grained reports, and explore additional features of the platform. We envision that SQLyzr facilitates the evaluation and iterative improvement of text-to-SQL models by addressing key limitations of existing benchmarks. The source code of SQLyzr is available at https://github.com/sepideh-abedini/SQLyzr.

</details>


### [23] [An Alternate Agentic AI Architecture (It's About the Data)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21413)
*Fabian Wenz,Felix Treutwein,Kai Arenja,Çagatay Demiralp,Michael Stonebraker*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文批评了当前将大语言模型作为智能体编排核心的主流方法，指出企业真正需要的是数据集成解决方案而非更强的推理能力。作者提出RUBICON架构，通过引入显式查询语言AQL和源包装器，将复杂问题分解为结构化、可审计的查询计划，从而在企业环境中实现可追溯、确定且可信的智能体搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业面临的是数据集成问题而非推理缺陷。现有基于LLM的架构针对非结构化文本推理优化，与模式丰富、受管控、性能关键的企业数据系统不匹配，导致透明度有限、正确性保证弱和性能不可预测。

Method: 提出RUBICON架构，引入AQL（智能体查询语言），这是一个小而显式的查询代数（Find, From, Where），通过源特定包装器执行，强制实施访问控制、模式对齐和结果归一化。复杂问题被分解为结构化的、可审计的查询计划，而非隐藏的LLM调用链。

Result: RUBICON架构通过显式查询代数替代不透明智能体编排，实现了可追溯、确定且可信的企业AI搜索。

Conclusion: 企业AI不是提示工程问题，而是系统问题。通过引入显式查询结构、基于包装器的中介和基于成本的优化，RUBICON在保留企业环境所需可追溯性、确定性和可信度的同时，实现了智能体搜索的广度。

Abstract: For the last several years, the dominant narrative in "agentic AI" has been that large language models should orchestrate information access by dynamically selecting tools, issuing sub-queries, and synthesizing results. We argue this approach is misguided: enterprises do not suffer from a reasoning deficit, but from a data integration problem.
  Enterprises are data-centric: critical information is scattered across heterogeneous systems (e.g., databases, documents, and external services), each with its own query language, schema, access controls, and performance constraints. In contrast, contemporary LLM-based architectures are optimized for reasoning over unstructured text and treat enterprise systems as either corpora or external tools invoked by a black-box component. This creates a mismatch between schema-rich, governed, performance-critical data systems and text-centric, probabilistic LLM architectures, leading to limited transparency, weak correctness guarantees, and unpredictable performance.
  In this paper, we present RUBICON, an alternative architecture grounded in data management principles. Instead of delegating orchestration to an opaque agent, we introduce AQL (Agentic Query Language), a small, explicit query algebra - Find, From, and Where - executed through source-specific wrappers that enforce access control, schema alignment, and result normalization. All intermediate results are visible and inspectable. Complex questions are decomposed into structured, auditable query plans rather than hidden chains of LLM calls.
  Our thesis is simple: enterprise AI is not a prompt engineering problem; it is a systems problem. By reintroducing explicit query structure, wrapper-based mediation, and cost-based optimization, we obtain the breadth of agentic search while preserving traceability, determinism, and trust in enterprise environments.

</details>

### [24] [Neuromorphic Computing Based on Parametrically-Driven Oscillators and Frequency Combs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21861)
*Mahadev Sunil Kumar,Adarsh Ganesan*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种具有2:1参量共振的双模系统，将其作为储备池计算平台，在亚阈值、参量共振和频率梳等不同动力学状态下对混沌系统进行一步预测。研究发现，参量共振状态下计算性能最优，而频率梳状态性能不稳定且混沌梳状态因相位相干性丧失而退化。预测误差与分岔结构直接对应，低误差区域沿参量共振边界分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参量驱动振荡器为神经形态计算提供天然平台，但不同动力学状态（亚阈值、参量共振、频率梳）对计算性能的影响尚不明确，缺乏系统的设计原则来优化振荡器储备池计算。

Method: 构建具有2:1参量共振的双模系统，通过将输入信号编码为驱动幅度并采样时域和频域响应，对Mackey-Glass、Rossler和Lorenz混沌系统进行一步预测。通过映射参数空间中的预测误差，分析不同动力学状态下的计算性能及其与分岔结构的关系。

Result: 参量共振状态下计算性能最优，非线性相互作用被激活且时间相干性得以保持；频率梳状态尽管光谱维数增加，但性能不稳定且在混沌梳区域因相位相干性丧失而退化；低误差区域与参量共振边界对齐，建立了计算能力与分岔结构之间的直接对应关系。

Conclusion: 参量共振是振荡器储备池计算的稳健工作状态，输入调制、失谐量、阻尼比和数据率系统性地控制可访问的动力学状态及计算性能，为物理系统调优以实现最佳神经形态功能提供了设计原则。

Abstract: Parametrically driven oscillators provide a natural platform for neuromorphic computation, where nonlinear mode coupling and intrinsic dynamics enable both memory and high-dimensional transformation. Here, we investigate a two-mode system exhibiting 2:1 parametric resonance and demonstrate its operation as a reservoir computer across distinct dynamical regimes, including sub-threshold, parametric resonance, and frequency-comb states. By encoding input signals into the drive amplitude and sampling the resulting temporal and spectral responses, we perform one step-ahead prediction of benchmark chaotic systems, including Mackey-Glass, Rossler, and Lorenz dynamics. We find that optimal computational performance is achieved within the parametric resonance regime, where nonlinear interactions are activated while temporal coherence is preserved. In contrast, although frequency-comb states introduce increased spectral dimensionality, their performance is not consistently good across their existence band and also degrades in the chaotic comb regime due to loss of phase coherence. Mapping prediction error over parameter space reveals a direct correspondence between computational capability and the underlying bifurcation structure, with low-error regions aligned with the parametric resonance boundary. We further show that the input modulation, the detuning from the frequency matching condition, damping ratio, and input data rate systematically control the accessible dynamical regimes and thereby the computational performance. These results establish parametric resonance as a robust operating regime for oscillator-based reservoir computing and provide design principles for tuning physical systems toward optimal neuromorphic functionality.

</details>

### [25] [Design, Modelling and Experimental Evaluation of a Tendon-driven Wrist Abduction-Adduction Mechanism for an upper limb exoskeleton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20893)
*Juwairiya S. Khan,Mostafa Mohammadi,John Rasmussen,Lotte N. S. Andreasen Struijk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于腕部外展-内收的单线缆（肌腱）螺旋扭簧辅助驱动机构，并建立基于仿真的刚度参数选择方法。通过运动学和动力学建模估算所需扭矩并优化弹簧参数，制作原型并在五名无运动障碍参与者中验证。结果表明，仿真指导的刚度选择能有效指导紧凑型线缆驱动腕部外骨骼设计，减少对经验调参的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统腕部外骨骼采用电机或气动等驱动方式，带来额外重量、摩擦和系统复杂性，亟需更轻量、简洁的驱动方案。

Method: 提出单线缆螺旋扭簧辅助驱动机构，使用单根Bowden线缆配合螺旋扭簧（时钟弹簧）被动张紧以维持连续线缆张力，无需拮抗驱动；建立运动学和动力学模型估算所需扭矩并优化弹簧参数；制作原型并在五种臂位和负载条件下对五名无运动障碍参与者进行三组弹簧配置的实验评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，仿真趋势与实验结果一致，标称弹簧配置在运动范围、扭矩需求和可重复性之间取得平衡。

Conclusion: 仿真指导的刚度选择可有效指导紧凑型线缆驱动腕部外骨骼的设计，减少对经验调试的依赖。

Abstract: Wrist exoskeletons play a vital role in rehabilitation and assistive applications, yet conventional actuation mechanisms such as electric motors or pneumatics often introduce undesirable weight, friction, and complexity. This paper presents a novel single-cable (tendon), torsional-spring-assisted actuation mechanism for wrist abduction-adduction, and a simulation-based method for selecting its stiffness parameters. The mechanism employs a single Bowden cable passively tensioned by a spiral torsional spring (clock spring) to maintain continuous cable tension without antagonistic actuation. Kinematic and dynamic modeling of the mechanism was performed to estimate the required torque and identify optimal spring parameters. These simulation-derived parameters guided the design of a functional prototype, which was experimentally evaluated with five participants with no motor disabilities (NMD) under varying arm positions and loading conditions using three spring configurations to account for user variability and modeling uncertainties. Experimental results show consistent agreement with simulation-derived trends, with the nominal spring configuration achieving balanced motion range, torque demand, and repeatability. The results demonstrate that simulation-informed stiffness selection can effectively guide the design of compact, cable-driven wrist exoskeletons while reducing reliance on empirical tuning.

</details>

*Juwairiya S. Khan,Mostafa Mohammadi,Alexander L. Ammitzbøll,Ellen-Merete Hagen,Jakob Blicher,Izabella Obál,Ana S. S. Cardoso,Oguzhan Kirtas,Rasmus L. Kæseler,John Rasmussen,Lotte N. S. Andreasen Struijk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究评估了在五自由度上肢外骨骼机器人EXOTIC2中集成主动腕部外展-内收（Ab-Ad）关节对康复训练的影响。通过肌腱驱动外展和弹簧驱动内收的轻型腕部模块，在无运动障碍的8名成人和1名肌萎缩侧索硬化症（ALS）患者中完成了饮水与抓挠任务测试。结果显示，腕部Ab-Ad自由度显著改善了任务成功率：饮水任务溢出率从56%降至3%，抓挠任务水平对齐成功率从28%提升至75%，且未增加执行时间。该研究为主动腕部Ab-Ad控制在辅助日常生活活动中的效能提供了实验证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上肢外骨骼在康复中缺乏对腕部外展-内收（Ab-Ad）关节功能影响的充分实验证据，尤其针对严重运动障碍患者。

Method: 将肌腱驱动外展、弹簧驱动内收的轻型腕部模块集成至EXOTIC五自由度上肢外骨骼；在8名无运动障碍成人（随机化腕部启用/锁定条件）及1名ALS患者中执行饮水与抓挠任务，评估腕部活动范围、任务完成时间、溢出率及水平对齐等指标。

Result: 腕部Ab-Ad自由度显著改善任务成功指标：饮水任务溢出发生率从56%降至3%；抓挠任务水平对齐成功率从28%提升至75%，且执行时间未增加。

Conclusion: 集成主动腕部Ab-Ad辅助可改善关键功能性任务结果，且不增加执行时间，为外骨骼辅助日常生活活动中主动腕部控制的效能提供了实验证据。

Abstract: Wrist function is essential in performing activities of daily living (ADLs). However, there is limited experimental evidence on the functional impact of wrist Abduction-Adduction (Ab-Ad) joint assistance in upper limb exoskeletons (ULEs) for rehabilitation. This study evaluates the effect of implementing an active wrist Ab-Ad joint in a five degree of freedom (DoF) ULE, EXOTIC2 exoskeleton, to support individuals with severe motor impairments. Methods: A compact, lightweight wrist module with tendon-driven abduction and spring-driven adduction was integrated into the EXOTIC exoskeleton. Eight adults with no motor disabilities completed drinking and scratching tasks under randomized wrist-enabled and wrist-locked conditions along with a preliminary feasibility test in one individual with Amyotrophic lateral sclerosis (ALS). Kinematic and task performance metrics including wrist range of motion, task completion time, spillage and leveling metrics were assessed. Results: Implementing the wrist Ab-Ad DoF improved task success metrics. Spill incidence during the drinking task decreased from 56% to 3%, and leveling success for scratching task improved from 28% to 75%. Conclusion: Integrating wrist Ab-Ad assistance improved key functional task outcomes without increasing execution time. Significance: The study provides the experimental evidence that active wrist Ab-Ad control enhances task-level performance in exoskeleton-assisted ADLs.

</details>

*Juwairiya S. Khan,Mostafa Mohammadi,Alexander L. Ammitzbøll,Ellen-Merete Hagen,Jakob Blicher Izabella Obál,Ana S. S. Cardoso,Oguzhan Kirtas,Rasmus L. Kæseler,John Rasmussen,Lotte N. S. Andreasen Struijk*

TL;DR: 本研究评估了腕部外展-内收（Ab-Ad）自由度辅助在舌控6自由度上肢外骨骼（EXOTIC2）中对ALS和SCI患者执行日常生活活动（饮水、刮痒棒调平）的功能影响。结果显示，启用腕部Ab-Ad辅助显著提高了任务成功率（溢出率从77.8%降至22.2%，放置失败率从66.7%降至16.7%），且未增加不适感，但效果依赖于任务情境和用户策略。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上肢外骨骼虽有望恢复重度运动功能障碍者的功能独立性，但腕部自由度（尤其是外展-内收）的临床相关性尚未得到充分评估。

Method: 采用受试者内设计，纳入1名肌萎缩侧索硬化症患者和5名脊髓损伤患者，在舌控6-DoF上肢外骨骼EXOTIC2上对比有无腕部Ab-Ad辅助两种条件，执行饮水和刮痒棒调平任务，评估任务成功率、完成时间、运动学指标及可用性问卷。

Result: 启用腕部Ab-Ad辅助使两种ADL的任务成功率均提升，溢出率从77.8%降至22.2%，放置失败率从66.7%降至16.7%；参与者使用了任务特异性的腕部运动范围子集；问卷显示无不适感增加且感知到任务表现改善。

Conclusion: 腕部Ab-Ad辅助可增强辅助外骨骼的功能任务表现且不损害用户舒适性，但效果依赖于任务情境、控制可用性和个体策略；本研究为在ULE中纳入腕部Ab-Ad提供了临床相关的、以用户为中心的证据支持。

Abstract: Upper-limb exoskeletons (ULEs) have the potential to restore functional independence in individuals with severe motor impairments; however, the clinical relevance of wrist degrees of freedom (DoF), particularly abduction-adduction (Ab-Ad), remains insufficiently evaluated. This study investigates the functional and user-perceived impact of wrist Ab-Ad assistance during two activities of daily living (ADLs). Wrist Ab-Ad assistance in a tongue-controlled 6-DoF ULE, EXOTIC2, was evaluated in a within-subject study involving one individual with amyotrophic lateral sclerosis and five individuals with spinal cord injury. Participants performed drinking and scratch stick leveling tasks with EXOTIC2 under two conditions: with and without wrist Ab-Ad assistance. Outcome measure included task success, task completion time, kinematic measures, and a usability questionnaire capturing comfort, functional perception, and acceptance. Enabling wrist Ab-Ad improved task success rates across both ADLs, with consistent reductions in spillage (from 77.8% spillages to 22.2%) and failed placements (from 66.7% to 16.7%). Participants utilized task-specific subsets of the available wrist range of motion, indicating that effective control within functional ranges was more critical than maximal joint excursion. Questionnaire responses indicated no increase in discomfort with the additional DoF and reflected perceived improvements in task performance. In conclusion, wrist Ab-Ad assistance enhances functional task performance in assistive exoskeleton use without compromising user comfort. However, its effectiveness depends on task context, control usability, and individual user strategies. This study provides clinically relevant, user-centered evidence supporting the inclusion of wrist Ab-Ad in ULEs, emphasizing the importance of balancing functional capability with usability in assistive device design.

</details>


### [28] [A Survey of Legged Robotics in Non-Inertial Environments: Past, Present, and Future](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20990)
*I-Chia Chang,Xinyan Huang,Tzu-Yuan Lin,Sangli Teng,Wenjing Li,Maani Ghaffari,Jingang Yi,Yan Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 腿足机器人在非惯性环境（移动、倾斜或加速的地面）中的 locomotion 可靠性仍有限。本文综述了该领域在建模、状态估计和控制方面的最新进展，总结了应用场景、性能退化原因、现有方法及其假设与局限，并指出了开放问题与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿足机器人在刚性静止地面上已展现出卓越敏捷性，但在非惯性环境（如地面运输、海洋平台、航空航天等移动/倾斜/加速地面）中，其运动可靠性严重受限。传统腿足 locomotion 的静止地面假设被打破，亟需系统梳理该新兴领域的技术基础。

Method: 本文采用综述方法：总结代表性应用领域与运动特性，分析 locomotion 性能退化的根本原因，回顾现有建模、状态估计与控制方法及其关键假设与局限性，并识别开放问题。

Result: 系统总结了非惯性环境中腿足机器人性能退化的根因（持续时变扰动），梳理了现有方法的主要假设与局限，识别了机器人-环境耦合、可观性、鲁棒性和实验验证等方面的开放问题。

Conclusion: 该综述阐明了非惯性环境腿足 locomotion 的技术基础，指明了自主性、系统级设计、仿生策略、安全性和测试等未来方向，旨在推动腿足机器人在真实动态环境中的可靠发展。

Abstract: Legged robots have demonstrated remarkable agility on rigid, stationary ground, but their locomotion reliability remains limited in non-inertial environments, where the supporting ground moves, tilts, or accelerates. Such conditions arise in ground transportation, maritime platforms, and aerospace settings, and they introduce persistent time-varying disturbances that break the stationary-ground assumptions underlying conventional legged locomotion. This survey reviews the state of the art in modeling, state estimation, and control for legged robots in non-inertial environments. We summarize representative application domains and motion characteristics, analyze the root causes of locomotion performance degradation, and review existing methods together with their key assumptions and limitations. We further identify open problems in robot-environment coupling, observability, robustness, and experimental validation, and discuss future directions in autonomy, system-level design, bio-inspired strategies, safety, and testing. The survey aims to clarify the technical foundations of this emerging area and support the development of reliable legged robots for real-world dynamic environments.

</details>
