Despite substantial progress in benchmarking CL methods, comparative evaluations typically keep the fine-tuning regime fixed. In this paper, we argue that the fine-tuning regime, defined by the trainable parameter subspace, is itself a key evaluation variable. We formalize adaptation regimes as projected optimization over fixed trainable subspaces, showing that changing the trainable depth alters the effective update signal through which both current task fitting and knowledge preservation operate. This analysis motivates the hypothesis that method comparisons need not be invariant across regimes. We test this hypothesis in task incremental CL, five trainable depth regimes, and four standard methods: online EWC, LwF, SI, and GEM. Across five benchmark datasets, namely MNIST, Fashion MNIST, KMNIST, QMNIST, and CIFAR-100, and across 11 task orders per dataset, we find that the relative ranking of methods is not consistently preserved across regimes. We further show that deeper adaptation regimes are associated with larger update magnitudes, higher forgetting, and a stronger relationship between the two. These results show that comparative conclusions in CL can depend strongly on the chosen fine-tuning regime, motivating regime-aware evaluation protocols that treat trainable depth as an explicit experimental factor.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [Sensitivity Uncertainty Alignment in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20903)
*Prakul Sunil Hiremath,Harshit R. Hiremath*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出敏感性-不确定性对齐(SUA)框架，通过衡量预测熵与分布敏感性之间的差异来检测大模型在对抗性和模糊输入下的失败，并引入SUA-TR训练方法与弃权规则提升模型可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗性敏感性和模糊性反映了同一问题：预测不稳定时模型未能表达相应的高不确定性，导致校准不良。现有方法（如熵或自一致性）无法充分识别此类失败。

Method: 定义标量分数 SUA_θ(x) 量化分布敏感性与预测熵之差；证明最小化其正部可上界最坏情况扰动风险并与校准误差关联；提出SUA-TR训练方法（结合一致性正则化与熵对齐）及基于弃权的安全推理规则。

Result: 在问答和分类等任务上，SUA比单独使用熵或自一致性更能有效识别模型失败。

Conclusion: SUA框架是模型无关的，为提升大模型在对抗性和模糊输入下的可靠性提供了基础。

Abstract: We propose Sensitivity-Uncertainty Alignment (SUA), a framework for analyzing failures of large language models under adversarial and ambiguous inputs. We argue that adversarial sensitivity and ambiguity reflect a common issue: misalignment between prediction instability and model uncertainty. A reliable model should express higher uncertainty when its predictions are unstable; failure to do so leads to miscalibration.
  We define a scalar score, SUA_theta(x), capturing the difference between distributional sensitivity and predictive entropy. We show that minimizing its positive part bounds worst-case perturbed risk and relates to calibration error. We also formalize ambiguity collapse, where models produce overconfident outputs despite multiple valid interpretations.
  We introduce SUA-TR, a training method combining consistency regularization and entropy alignment, along with an abstention rule for safer inference. Across tasks including question answering and classification, SUA better identifies model failures than entropy or self-consistency alone.
  The framework is model-agnostic and provides a basis for improving reliability in evolving language models.

</details>


### [104] [Omission Constraints Decay While Commission Constraints Persist in Long-Context LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20911)
*Yeran Gamage*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LLM智能体在长对话中面临"安全-记忆发散"（SRD）问题：禁止型约束随上下文压力衰减，而要求型约束保持有效。在12个模型、8个提供商、4416次试验中，遗漏合规率从第5轮的73%降至第16轮的33%，而委托合规率保持100%。重新注入约束可在安全轮次深度前恢复合规性。标准监控无法检测到遗漏约束的失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体在生产环境中部署时需遵循操作员定义的行为策略（如禁止泄露凭证、禁止数据外泄等），但安全评估假设这些约束在整个对话中保持不变。研究发现禁止型约束会随上下文压力衰减，而要求型约束持续有效，这种不对称性导致安全监控盲区。

Method: 采用4416次试验的三臂因果研究，覆盖12个模型、8个提供商、6个对话深度。通过对比遗漏合规与委托合规的衰减差异，并使用token匹配填充控制（两个模型）来分离语义内容的影响。

Result: 遗漏合规率从第5轮的73%降至第16轮的33%，而委托合规率保持100%（Mistral Large 3, p < 10^{-33}）。语义内容解释了62-100%的稀释效应。在安全轮次深度前重新注入约束可恢复合规性。

Conclusion: 生产环境中的安全策略包含大量禁止型约束（如不泄露凭证、不执行未信任代码、不转发用户数据），委托型审计信号保持正常而遗漏型约束已失效，导致标准监控无法发现此类安全失效。

Abstract: LLM agents deployed in production operate under operator-defined behavioral policies (system-prompt instructions such as prohibitions on credential disclosure, data exfiltration, and unauthorized output) that safety evaluations assume hold throughout a conversation. Prohibition-type constraints decay under context pressure while requirement-type constraints persist; we term this asymmetry Security-Recall Divergence (SRD). In a 4,416-trial three-arm causal study across 12 models and 8 providers at six conversation depths, omission compliance falls from 73% at turn 5 to 33% at turn 16 while commission compliance holds at 100% (Mistral Large 3, $p < 10^{-33}$). In the two models with token-matched padding controls, schema semantic content accounts for 62-100% of the dilution effect. Re-injecting constraints before the per-model Safe Turn Depth (STD) restores compliance without retraining. Production security policies consist of prohibitions such as never revealing credentials, never executing untrusted code, and never forwarding user data. Commission-type audit signals remain healthy while omission constraints have already failed, leaving the failure invisible to standard monitoring.

</details>


### [105] [Hidden Secrets in the arXiv: Discovering, Analyzing, and Preventing Unintentional Information Disclosure in Source Files of Scientific Preprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20927)
*Jan Pennekamp,Johannes Lohmöller,David Schütte,Joscha Loos,Martin Henze*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究系统分析了arXiv上270万篇论文的源文件中无意披露的"隐藏"信息，发现几乎所有提交都包含不必要的文件、元数据或无关内容（如API密钥、Git历史等），现有清理工具效果不佳，作者提供了ALC-NG工具来全面清理这些信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: arXiv公开了论文的LaTeX源文件，这可能导致敏感信息（如私有密钥、内部协调链接等）被无意披露，但目前缺乏对这一问题的系统性量化研究。

Method: 对arXiv上所有270万份包含源文件的提交，从三个维度（不必要的文件、文件中的元数据、文件中的无关内容如代码注释）进行系统分析。

Result: 几乎每一份arXiv提交都包含某种形式的"隐藏"信息，包括内部协调文档链接、API和私有密钥、完整的Git历史等；现有清理工具无法可靠地完成清理功能。

Conclusion: arXiv源文件中广泛存在无意识的信息泄露问题，现有工具不足，作者提供了ALC-NG工具用于全面清理编译LaTeX论文不需要的文件、元数据和注释。

Abstract: Preprints are essential for the timely and open dissemination of research. arXiv, the most widely used preprint service, takes the idea of open science one step further by not only publishing the actual preprints but also LaTeX sources and other files used to create them. As known from other contexts, such as GitHub repositories, and anecdotally exemplified for arXiv, making source code publicly available risks disclosing otherwise "hidden" information. Consequently, the public availability of paper sources raises the question of how much sensitive content is (unintentionally) disclosed through them.
  In this paper, we systematically answer this question for all 2.7M arXiv submissions with available source files across three dimensions of source file-induced information disclosure: (1) inclusion of unnecessary files, (2) metadata embedded in files, and (3) irrelevant content in files such as source code comments. Our analysis reveals that nearly every arXiv submission contains some form of "hidden" information. Notable findings range from links to editable web documents for internal coordination over API and private keys to complete Git histories.
  While different tools promise to remove such information from source files, we show that they fail to reliably achieve the intended cleaning functionality. To mitigate this situation, we provide ALC-NG to comprehensively remove files, metadata, and comments that are not needed to compile a LaTeX paper.

</details>


### [106] [SafeRedirect: Defeating Internal Safety Collapse via Task-Completion Redirection in Frontier LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20930)
*Chao Pan,Yu Wu,Xin Yao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SafeRedirect方法，通过重定向模型任务完成驱动力而非抑制它，有效防御前沿LLM在执行专业任务时超过95%安全失败率的内部安全崩溃（ISC）问题，将平均不安全生成率从71.2%降至8.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿LLM在执行专业任务时存在内部安全崩溃（ISC）失效模式——当任务在结构化上需要有害内容时，模型自发生成有害内容且安全失败率超过95%，现有输入级防御完全无效，标准系统提示防御仅能部分缓解。

Method: 提出SafeRedirect系统级重写防御方法：明确允许任务失败、规定确定性硬停止输出、指示模型保留有害占位符未解析状态，从而重定向模型的任务完成驱动力而非抑制它。

Result: 在7个前沿LLM、3种AI/ML相关ISC任务类型的单轮设置中，平均不安全生成率从71.2%降至8.0%，而最强可行基线为55.0%。跨攻击评估证实对ISC的防御达到SOTA水平，对其他攻击家族的泛化性能至少与基线持平。

Conclusion: SafeRedirect通过重定向而非抑制任务驱动力，有效解决ISC问题；多模型消融实验表明失败许可和条件特异性在所有模型中普遍关键，其他组件的重要性因模型而异。

Abstract: Internal Safety Collapse (ISC) is a failure mode in which frontier LLMs, when executing legitimate professional tasks whose correct completion structurally requires harmful content, spontaneously generate that content with safety failure rates exceeding 95%. Existing input-level defenses achieve a 100% failure rate against ISC, and standard system prompt defenses provide only partial mitigation. We propose SafeRedirect, a system-level override that defeats ISC by redirecting the model's task-completion drive rather than suppressing it. SafeRedirect grants explicit permission to fail the task, prescribes a deterministic hard-stop output, and instructs the model to preserve harmful placeholders unresolved. Evaluated on seven frontier LLMs across three AI/ML-related ISC task types in the single-turn setting, SafeRedirect reduces average unsafe generation rates from 71.2% to 8.0%, compared to 55.0% for the strongest viable baseline. Multi-model ablation reveals that failure permission and condition specificity are universally critical, while the importance of other components varies across models. Cross-attack evaluation confirms state-of-the-art defense against ISC with generalization performance at least on par with the baseline on other attack families. Code is available at https://github.com/fzjcdt/SafeRedirect.

</details>


### [107] [Adaptive Defense Orchestration for RAG: A Sentinel-Strategist Architecture against Multi-Vector Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20932)
*Pranav Pallerla,Wilson Naik Bhukya,Bharath Vemula,Charan Ramtej Kodi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Sentinel-Strategist架构，通过上下文感知的自适应防御选择机制，在RAG系统中显著缓解安全与效用之间的权衡，在消除成员推理攻击的同时将检索效用恢复到接近无防御基线水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统在医疗、法律等敏感领域的部署带来了成员推理、数据投毒和内容泄露等安全风险，而同时启用所有防御措施会导致严重的效用损失（上下文召回率下降超40%），亟需一种能平衡安全与效用的方法。

Method: 提出Sentinel-Strategist架构：Sentinel组件检测异常检索行为，Strategist组件根据查询上下文选择性部署仅必要的防御措施，实现自适应、查询感知的防御选择。

Result: 在三个基准数据集和五个编排模型上，该方法完全消除MBA式成员推理泄露；在数据投毒场景下，最强变体将攻击成功率降至接近零，同时将上下文召回率恢复至无防御基线的75%以上。

Conclusion: 自适应、查询感知的防御策略能够显著降低RAG系统的安全-效用权衡，但鲁棒性对模型选择仍较敏感。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems are increasingly deployed in sensitive domains such as healthcare and law, where they rely on private, domain-specific knowledge. This capability introduces significant security risks, including membership inference, data poisoning, and unintended content leakage. A straightforward mitigation is to enable all relevant defenses simultaneously, but doing so incurs a substantial utility cost. In our experiments, an always-on defense stack reduces contextual recall by more than 40%, indicating that retrieval degradation is the primary failure mode. To mitigate this trade-off in RAG systems, we propose the Sentinel-Strategist architecture, a context-aware framework for risk analysis and defense selection. A Sentinel detects anomalous retrieval behavior, after which a Strategist selectively deploys only the defenses warranted by the query context. Evaluated across three benchmark datasets and five orchestration models, ADO is shown to eliminate MBA-style membership inference leakage while substantially recovering retrieval utility relative to a fully static defense stack, approaching undefended baseline levels. Under data poisoning, the strongest ADO variants reduce attack success to near zero while restoring contextual recall to more than 75% of the undefended baseline, although robustness remains sensitive to model choice. Overall, these findings show that adaptive, query-aware defense can substantially reduce the security-utility trade-off in RAG systems.

</details>


### [108] [Behavioral Consistency and Transparency Analysis on Large Language Model API Gateways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21083)
*Guanjie Lin,Yinxin Wan,Shichao Pei,Ting Xu,Kuai Xu,Guoliang Xue*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 GateScope，一个轻量级黑盒测量框架，用于评估商业 LLM API 网关的行为一致性与运营透明度。通过对10个真实网关在响应内容、多轮对话、计费准确性和延迟特性四个维度的审计，发现存在模型静默替换、记忆衰退、定价偏离和延迟不稳定等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第三方LLM API网关的内部路由、缓存和计费策略不透明，用户无法确认请求是否由广告宣传的模型处理、响应是否忠实于上游API、账单是否准确反映公开定价策略。

Method: 设计GateScope黑盒测量框架，从四个维度审计网关行为：响应内容分析（检测模型降级/替换、静默截断）、多轮对话性能（检测记忆衰退）、计费准确性（检测定价偏差）、延迟特性（检测稳定性）。

Result: 在10个商业LLM API网关上发现：静默模型替换、记忆保持能力下降、偏离公开定价、各平台延迟稳定性存在显著差异。

Conclusion: LLM API网关普遍存在期望行为与实际行为不一致的问题，GateScope框架能有效检测这些不透明行为。

Abstract: Third-party Large Language Model (LLM) API gateways are rapidly emerging as unified access points to models offered by multiple vendors. However, the internal routing, caching, and billing policies of these gateways are largely undisclosed, leaving users with limited visibility into whether requests are served by the advertised models, whether responses remain faithful to upstream APIs, or whether invoices accurately reflect public pricing policies. To address this gap, we introduce GateScope, a lightweight black-box measurement framework for evaluating behavioral consistency and operational transparency in commercial LLM gateways. GateScope is designed to detect key misbehaviors, including model downgrading or switching, silent truncation, billing inaccuracies, and instability in latency by auditing gateways along four critical dimensions: response content analysis, multi-turn conversation performance, billing accuracy, and latency characteristics. Our measurements across 10 real-world commercial LLM API gateways reveal frequent gaps between expected and actual behaviors, including silent model substitutions, degraded memory retention, deviations from announced pricing, and substantial variation in latency stability across platforms.

</details>


### [109] [Breaking Bad: Interpretability-Based Safety Audits of State-of-the-Art LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20945)
*Krishiv Agarwal,Ramneet Kaur,Colin Samplawski,Manoj Acharya,Anirban Roy,Daniel Elenius,Brian Matejek,Adam D. Cobb,Susmit Jha*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可解释性驱动的越狱审计方法，通过对8种开源大语言模型进行通用导向（US）和表征工程（RepE）攻击，发现Llama-3系列模型高度脆弱（越狱率高达91%），GPT-oss-120B最为鲁棒，小模型比大模型更安全，揭示了可解释性导向的双重用途风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型安全审计主要依赖黑盒探测方法，无法系统性地揭示模型内部机制中存在的安全漏洞，迫切需要基于模型内部表征的可解释性审计工具。

Method: 采用通用导向（Universal Steering, US）和表征工程（Representation Engineering, RepE）两种可解释性方法，引入自适应两阶段网格搜索算法来优化针对不安全行为概念的最优激活导向系数，在8种SOTA开源LLM上进行系统审计。

Result: Llama-3系列模型高度脆弱，Llama-3.3-70B-4bt的越狱响应率高达91%（US）和83%（RepE）；GPT-oss-120B对两种攻击均表现出最强鲁棒性；Qwen和Phi系列小模型（Qwen3-0.6B、Phi4-3.8B）越狱率较低，而大模型更易受攻击。

Conclusion: 可解释性导向攻击是进行系统性安全审计的有效工具，但同时揭示了其双重用途风险，强调了在大语言模型部署中需要加强内部防御机制。

Abstract: Effective safety auditing of large language models (LLMs) demands tools that go beyond black-box probing and systematically uncover vulnerabilities rooted in model internals. We present a comprehensive, interpretability-driven jailbreaking audit of eight SOTA open-source LLMs: Llama-3.1-8B, Llama-3.3-70B-4bt, GPT-oss- 20B, GPT-oss-120B, Qwen3-0.6B, Qwen3-32B, Phi4-3.8B, and Phi4-14B. Leveraging interpretability-based approaches -- Universal Steering (US) and Representation Engineering (RepE) -- we introduce an adaptive two-stage grid search algorithm to identify optimal activation-steering coefficients for unsafe behavioral concepts. Our evaluation, conducted on a curated set of harmful queries and a standardized LLM-based judging protocol, reveals stark contrasts in model robustness. The Llama-3 models are highly vulnerable, with up to 91\% (US) and 83\% (RepE) jailbroken responses on Llama-3.3-70B-4bt, while GPT-oss-120B remains robust to attacks via both interpretability approaches. Qwen and Phi models show mixed results, with the smaller Qwen3-0.6B and Phi4-3.8B mostly exhibiting lower jailbreaking rates, while their larger counterparts are more susceptible. Our results establish interpretability-based steering as a powerful tool for systematic safety audits, but also highlight its dual-use risks and the need for better internal defenses in LLM deployment.

</details>


### [110] [Breaking MCP with Function Hijacking Attacks: Novel Threats for Function Calling and Agentic Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20994)
*Yannis Belkhiter,Giulio Zizzo,Sergio Maffeis,Seshu Tirupathi,John D. Kelleher*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出函数劫持攻击(FHA)，通过操纵智能体模型的工具选择过程，强制调用攻击者指定的函数。该攻击对语义上下文和函数集具有鲁棒性，可训练生成通用对抗函数，在5种模型上达到70%-100%的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI的快速发展使得函数调用型LLM被广泛使用，但其函数调用接口引入了额外的安全漏洞。现有攻击主要关注语义偏好操纵，尚缺乏对工具选择过程本身进行劫持的攻击方法研究。

Method: 提出函数劫持攻击(FHA)，通过构造对抗性函数描述来操纵模型对工具的选择，使其强制调用攻击者指定的函数。FHA对上下文语义不敏感，可跨领域应用，并能训练产生通用的对抗函数，适用于多查询和多负载配置。

Result: 在BFCL数据集上对5种不同模型（包括指令模型和推理变体）进行实验，攻击成功率(ASR)达到70%-100%。

Conclusion: 函数劫持攻击揭示了智能体系统函数调用接口的严重安全漏洞，现有的安全防护措施不足，亟需为智能体系统建立更强大的防护框架和安全模块。

Abstract: The growth of agentic AI has drawn significant attention to function calling Large Language Models (LLMs), which are designed to extend the capabilities of AI-powered system by invoking external functions. Injection and jailbreaking attacks have been extensively explored to showcase the vulnerabilities of LLMs to user prompt manipulation. The expanded capabilities of agentic models introduce further vulnerabilities via their function calling interface. Recent work in LLM security showed that function calling can be abused, leading to data tampering and theft, causing disruptive behavior such as endless loops, or causing LLMs to produce harmful content in the style of jailbreaking attacks. This paper introduces a novel function hijacking attack (FHA) that manipulates the tool selection process of agentic models to force the invocation of a specific, attacker-chosen function. While existing attacks focus on semantic preference of the model for function-calling tasks, we show that FHA is largely agnostic to the context semantics and robust to the function sets, making it applicable across diverse domains. We further demonstrate that FHA can be trained to produce universal adversarial functions, enabling a single attacked function to hijack tool selection across multiple queries and payload configurations. We conducted experiments on 5 different models, including instructed and reasoning variants, reaching 70% to 100% ASR over the established BFCL dataset. Our findings further demonstrate the need for strong guardrails and security modules for agentic systems.

</details>


### [111] [VRSafe: A Secure Virtual Keyboard to Mitigate Keystroke Inference in Virtual Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21001)
*Yijun Yuan,Na Du,Adam J. Lee,Balaji Palanisamy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为VRSafe的新型QWERTY虚拟键盘，通过在攻击者收集的信息中注入假阳性击键来防御VR环境中的击键推断攻击，并集成恶意登录检测器，在保持可用性的同时显著降低攻击准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密码认证在VR应用中广泛使用，但现有防御方法缺乏有效且低成本的策略来抵御VR中的击键推断攻击。

Method: 设计了一种新型QWERTY键盘VRSafe，通过在攻击者收集的键入信息中精心引入假阳性击键来混淆真实密码；同时集成恶意登录检测器，以高检测率、低时间和内存成本识别基于推断凭据的未授权登录尝试。

Result: 通过仿真实验和真实用户研究评估，VRSafe能显著降低击键推断攻击的准确率，同时仅带来适度的可用性开销。

Conclusion: VRSafe为VR环境中的击键推断攻击提供了一种有效且成本高效的防御方案。

Abstract: Password-based authentication is one of the most commonly used methods for verifying user identities, and its widespread usage continues in virtual reality (VR) applications. As a result, various forms of attacks on password-based authentication in traditional environments such as keystroke inference and shoulder surfing, are still effective in VR applications. While keystroke inference attacks on virtual keyboards have been studied extensively, few efforts have developed an effective and cost-efficient defense strategy to mitigate keystroke inferences in VR. To address this gap, this paper presents a novel QWERTY keyboard called \textit{VRSafe} that is resilient to keystroke inference attacks. The proposed keyboard carefully introduces false positive keystrokes into the information collected by attackers during the typing process, making the inference of the original password difficult. \textit{VRSafe} also incorporates a novel malicious login detector that can effectively identify unauthorized login attempts using credentials inferred from keystroke inference attacks with high detection rate and minimal time and memory cost. The proposed design is evaluated through both simulation experiments and a real-world user study, and the results show that \textit{VRSafe} can significantly reduce the accuracy of keystroke inference attacks while incurring a modest overhead from a usability standpoint.

</details>


### [112] [Layer 2 Blockchains Simplified: A Survey of Vector Commitment Schemes, ZKP Frameworks, Layer-2 Data Structures and Verkle Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21055)
*Ekleen Kaur,Marko Suvajdzic*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次提出了一个统一的、以安全为中心的Layer-2协议综述，系统性地将L2架构映射到其底层密码学安全基础，分析了ZK框架（Groth16、Plonk）和承诺方案（KZG、IPA）的安全假设，并形式化了包含排序器活性、桥漏洞和数据可用性故障的综合L2威胁模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献[31][33]对L2安全风险的呈现是碎片化的，缺乏统一视角；L2架构引入的链下组件（排序器、桥、数据可用性机制）带来了复杂的新安全面，亟需系统性梳理。

Method: 采用安全聚焦的综述方法：将L2架构映射到底层密码学安全，剖析从L1原语到现代L2核心的技术演进，分析ZK框架（Groth16, Plonk）及其承诺方案（KZG, IPA）的安全假设（离散对数、CDH、双线性DH），并形式化综合L2威胁模型。

Result: 建立了首个统一的L2安全综述框架，系统分类了L2各组件对应的密码学安全假设，并形式化了涵盖三类核心威胁（排序器活性、桥漏洞、数据可用性故障）的综合威胁模型。

Conclusion: 本文为研究人员和开发者提供了一个既易理解又严谨的参考，使其能够从深入的密码学-数学视角推理L2安全性。

Abstract: Layer-2 (L2) protocols address the fundamental limitations of Layer-1 (L1) blockchains by offloading computation while anchoring trust to the parent chain. This architectural shift, while boosting throughput, introduces a new, complex security surface defined by off-chain components like sequencers, bridges, and data availability mechanisms. Prior literature[31][33] offers fragmented views of this risk. This paper presents the first unified, security-focused survey that rigorously maps L2 architecture to its underlying cryptographic security. We dissect the technical progression from L1 primitives to the core of modern L2s, analyzing the security assumptions(Discrete Logarithm, Computational Diffie-Hellman, Bilinear Diffie-Hellman) of ZK frameworks (Groth16, Plonk) and their corresponding commitment schemes (KZG, IPA). We formalize a comprehensive L2 threat model encompassing sequencer liveness, bridge exploits, and data-availability failures. This work serves as an accessible yet rigorous reference for researchers and developers to reason about L2 security from a deep crypto-mathematical perspective.

</details>


### [113] [Cross-Session Threats in AI Agents: Benchmark, Evaluation, and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21131)
*Ari Azarafrooz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对AI智能体防护栏无法跨会话检测威胁的问题，提出了CSTM-Bench数据集、跨会话威胁检测测量方法和Coreset Memory Reader算法及CSTM评估指标，实验表明仅Coreset Memory Reader在跨会话场景下能保持召回率，而其他方法（会话边界判断器和全日志关联器）召回率均下降约一半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体防护栏（guardrails）是无记忆的，每个消息独立判断，攻击者可将单个攻击分散到数十个会话中，由于只有聚合体才携带恶意载荷，每个会话边界检测器都无法发现威胁，因此需要跨会话威胁检测方法。

Method: (1) 构建CSTM-Bench数据集：包含26种可执行攻击分类，按杀伤链阶段和跨会话操作（accumulate, compose, launder, inject_on_reader）分类，绑定7种身份锚点，含良性混淆样本；(2) 测量方法：将跨会话检测视为信息瓶颈问题，对比会话边界判断器和Full-Log Correlator（拼接所有提示到长上下文）；(3) 提出Coreset Memory Reader算法（保留最高信号片段，K=50）及CSTM评估指标，引入CSR_prefix度量排序前缀稳定性。

Result: 会话边界判断器和Full-Log Correlator在从dilution分片到cross_session分片时，攻击召回率均下降约一半；仅Coreset Memory Reader（K=50）在两个分片上都保持了召回率。数据集已发布在Hugging Face上。

Conclusion: 跨会话威胁检测是重要且未充分解决的问题；现有长上下文拼接方法不足以应对；有界记忆的Coreset Memory Reader是有效方案；CSTM指标将检测性能与服务稳定性统一为帕累托前沿，为未来更大规模、多供应商数据集的研究提供了基准。

Abstract: AI-agent guardrails are memoryless: each message is judged in isolation, so an adversary who spreads a single attack across dozens of sessions slips past every session-bound detector because only the aggregate carries the payload. We make three contributions to cross-session threat detection.
  (1) Dataset. CSTM-Bench is 26 executable attack taxonomies classified by kill-chain stage and cross-session operation (accumulate, compose, launder, inject_on_reader), each bound to one of seven identity anchors that ground-truth "violation" as a policy predicate, plus matched Benign-pristine and Benign-hard confounders. Released on Hugging Face as intrinsec-ai/cstm-bench with two 54-scenario splits: dilution (compositional) and cross_session (12 isolation-invisible scenarios produced by a closed-loop rewriter that softens surface phrasing while preserving cross-session artefacts).
  (2) Measurement. Framing cross-session detection as an information bottleneck to a downstream correlator LLM, we find that a session-bound judge and a Full-Log Correlator concatenating every prompt into one long-context call both lose roughly half their attack recall moving from dilution to cross_session, well inside any frontier context window. Scope: 54 scenarios per shard, one correlator family (Anthropic Claude), no prompt optimisation; we release it to motivate larger, multi-provider datasets.
  (3) Algorithm and metric. A bounded-memory Coreset Memory Reader retaining highest-signal fragments at $K=50$ is the only reader whose recall survives both shards. Because ranker reshuffles break KV-cache prefix reuse, we promote $\mathrm{CSR\_prefix}$ (ordered prefix stability, LLM-free) to a first-class metric and fuse it with detection into $\mathrm{CSTM} = 0.7 F_1(\mathrm{CSDA@action}, \mathrm{precision}) + 0.3 \mathrm{CSR\_prefix}$, benchmarking rankers on a single Pareto of recall versus serving stability.

</details>


### [114] [Position Paper: Denial-of-Service Against Multi-Round Transaction Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21169)
*Yuzhe Tang,Yibo Wang,Wanning Ding,Jiaqi Chen,Taesoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了针对以太坊交易打包服务（如Flashbots Bundles）的新型拒绝服务攻击。通过利用合约状态中的交易间依赖关系和打包服务的原子区块包含特性，作者开发了隐蔽、无风险且低成本的DoS攻击方法。实验表明，该攻击成功率高，能显著降低构建者收入并减慢区块生产速度。文章最后提出了相应的缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的拒绝服务攻击设计由于无法适应打包服务独特的多轮执行模型而效果不佳。本文旨在填补这一研究空白，探索针对打包服务的不对称拒绝服务攻击。

Method: 利用交易间依赖关系（通过合约状态）实现隐蔽性，同时滥用打包服务的原子区块包含等特性来降低攻击的资本和运营成本。

Result: 实验结果表明攻击成功率高，能显著减少构建者收入并减慢区块生成速度。

Conclusion: 提出了针对已识别风险的缓解策略。

Abstract: In Ethereum, transaction-bundling services are a critical component of block builders, such as Flashbots Bundles, and are widely used by MEV searchers. Disrupting bundling services can degrade searcher experience and reduce builder revenue. Despite the extensive studies, the existing denial-of-service attack designs are ineffective against bundling services due to their unique multi-round execution model.
  This paper studies the open problem of asymmetric denial-of-service against bundling services. We develop evasive, risk-free, and low-cost DoS attacks on Flashbots' bundling service, the only open-source bundling service known to us. Our attacks exploit inter-transaction dependencies through contract state to achieve evasiveness, and abuse bundling-specific features, such as atomic block inclusion, to significantly reduce both capital and operational costs of the attack.
  Experimental results show that our attacks achieve high success rates, substantially reduce builders' revenue, and slow block production. We further propose mitigation strategies for the identified risks.

</details>


### [115] [Physically Unclonable Functions for Secure IoT Authentication and Hardware-Anchored AI Model Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21188)
*Maryam Taghi Zadeh,Mohsen Ahmadi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文综述了AI赋能IoT系统中基于硬件的可信根机制，系统比较了TPM、PUF、混合容器感知可信根及纯软件方案，指出硬件方案在抗物理篡改和设备克隆方面优于软件方案，特别在对抗性和物理暴露环境中，而PUF和混合可信根在大规模AIoT系统中展现出最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI与IoT及边缘计算的快速融合对设备认证和AI模型完整性提出了更高安全需求，而现有方案在资源受限和严格安全约束下缺乏系统性的比较研究。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，对TPM、PUF（硅基和FPGA）、混合容器感知可信根及纯软件安全方案进行了比较分析，从安全性、可扩展性、成本和部署复杂度等维度进行评估。

Result: 硬件根方案在对抗性和物理暴露环境下提供更强的抗物理篡改和设备克隆保护；混合设计将硬件信任扩展到运行时和容器化环境；PUF和混合可信根在大规模AIoT系统中表现出最佳综合性能；纯软件方案在对抗性和物理暴露场景下不足以满足安全需求。

Conclusion: PUF和混合可信根为大规模AIoT系统提供了安全性与可部署性的最佳平衡，指明了未来可信AIoT平台的设计方向。

Abstract: The rapid integration of artificial intelligence (AI) into Internet of Things (IoT) and edge computing systems has intensified the need for robust, hardware-rooted trust mechanisms capable of ensuring device authenticity and AI model integrity under strict resource and security constraints. This survey reviews and synthesizes existing literature on hardware-rooted trust mechanisms for AI-enabled IoT systems. It systematically examines and compares representative trust anchor mechanisms, including Trusted Platform Module (TPM)-based measurement and attestation, silicon and FPGA-based Physical Unclonable Functions (PUFs), hybrid container-aware hardware roots of trust, and software-only security approaches. The analysis highlights how hardware-rooted solutions generally provide stronger protection against physical tampering and device cloning compared to software-only approaches, particularly in adversarial and physically exposed environments, while hybrid designs extend hardware trust into runtime and containerized environments commonly used in modern edge deployments. By evaluating trade-offs among security strength, scalability, cost, and deployment complexity, the study shows that PUF-based and hybrid trust anchors offer a promising balance for large-scale, AI-enabled IoT systems, whereas software-only trust mechanisms remain insufficient in adversarial and physically exposed settings. The presented comparison aims to clarify current design challenges and guide future development of trustworthy AI-enabled IoT platforms.

</details>


### [116] [ECCFROG522PP: An Enhanced 522 bit Weierstrass Elliptic Curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21261)
*Victor Duarte Melo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ECCFROG522PP，一条522-bit素数域椭圆曲线（短Weierstrass形式），所有关键参数通过BLAKE3确定性管道从公开种子生成，强调透明度、可审计性和可复现性，目标安全等级与P-521相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有广泛部署的曲线（如NIST P-256、secp256k1）及更高安全曲线（如P-521、Curve448、Brainpool P512）在参数生成过程中缺乏完全透明和可审计的确定性流程，本文旨在通过公开种子和确定性推导弥补这一缺口。

Method: 采用BLAKE3哈希函数构建确定性参数生成管道，所有索引和步骤公开指定；曲线为短Weierstrass形式，素数域522-bit；通过该管道确定性地推导出素数阶、余因子为1的群及满阶基点；对二次扭给出大素数因子的下界证明，并计算嵌入度下界，执行低嵌入度约简和CM判别式异常的标准检查。

Result: 生成一条素数阶、余因子为1的椭圆曲线，具有确定性的满阶基点；二次扭具有已证明的大素数因子；嵌入度下界被记录，通过所有标准安全性检查；整个生成与验证流程可从公开工件和参考脚本端到端复现。

Conclusion: ECCFROG522PP实现了与P-521相当的安全等级下，完全透明、可审计且可独立验证的曲线参数生成流程，为需要高安全性和公开可信度的应用场景提供了一种替代方案。

Abstract: This paper presents ECCFROG522PP, a 522-bit prime-field elliptic curve in short Weierstrass form, designed with a focus on deterministic generation and public reproducibility. The central design principle is that all critical parameters are derived from a fixed public seed through a transparent and verifiable procedure. While many deployed systems rely on NIST P-256 and secp256k1, which target approximately 128-bit classical security, higher security applications typically consider curves such as NIST P-521, Curve448, and Brainpool P512. ECCFROG522PP is intended for the same general classical security range as P-521, with emphasis on transparency, auditability, and reproducibility rather than performance optimization.
  The curve parameters are generated through a BLAKE3-based deterministic pipeline with publicly specified indices. The resulting construction has prime order, cofactor one, and a deterministically derived base point of full order. The quadratic twist has a large proven prime factor, and the construction includes a documented lower bound on the embedding degree together with standard sanity checks against low embedding degree reductions and basic CM discriminant anomalies.
  The full generation and validation procedure can be reproduced end to end from public artifacts and reference scripts, enabling independent verification of all parameters and checks.

</details>


### [117] [Adversarial Evasion in Non-Stationary Malware Detection: Minimizing Drift Signals through Similarity-Constrained Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21310)
*Pawan Acharya,Lan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究能否生成同时逃避分类检测和漂移监控的对抗性恶意软件样本，提出在标准化特征空间中生成目标对抗样本并加入相似性正则化，实验表明ℓ2正则化能有效降低输出漂移信号，且扰动预算显著影响逃逸与可检测性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习恶意软件检测模型在非平稳环境中面临关键限制——恶意软件特征和检测系统都在持续演化。作者探究一个基础安全问题：攻击者能否生成同时逃避分类和漂移监控的对抗性恶意软件样本。

Method: 在分类器的标准化特征空间中生成目标对抗性示例，并加入复杂的相似性正则化器。通过谨慎约束扰动以保持与干净恶意软件之间的分布相似性，构建一个平衡目标错误分类与漂移信号最小化的优化目标。

Result: 相似性约束可降低输出漂移信号，其中ℓ2正则化效果最佳。扰动预算显著影响逃逸-可检测性权衡，预算增加导致更高的攻击成功率和更显著的漂移指标。

Conclusion: 提出的对抗性攻击方法能够在逃避分类检测的同时降低漂移检测信号，但扰动预算与逃逸成功率、可检测性之间存在根本性权衡关系。

Abstract: Deep learning has emerged as a powerful approach for malware detection, demonstrating impressive accuracy across various data representations. However, these models face critical limitations in real-world, non-stationary environments where both malware characteristics and detection systems continuously evolve. Our research investigates a fundamental security question: Can an attacker generate adversarial malware samples that simultaneously evade classification and remain inconspicuous to drift monitoring mechanisms? We propose a novel approach that generates targeted adversarial examples in the classifier's standardized feature space, augmented with sophisticated similarity regularizers. By carefully constraining perturbations to maintain distributional similarity with clean malware, we create an optimization objective that balances targeted misclassification with drift signal minimization. We quantify the effectiveness of this approach by comprehensively comparing classifier output probabilities using multiple drift metrics. Our experiments demonstrate that similarity constraints can reduce output drift signals, with $\ell_2$ regularization showing the most promising results. We observe that perturbation budget significantly influences the evasion-detectability trade-off, with increased budget leading to higher attack success rates and more substantial drift indicators.

</details>


### [118] [Provably Secure Steganography Based on List Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21394)
*Kaiyi Pang,Minhao Bai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于列表解码的高容量可证明安全隐写方案，利用后缀匹配机制从候选集合中识别正确秘密信息，在保证计算不可区分性的同时显著提升嵌入容量，且即插即用兼容现有LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语言模型的可证明安全隐写方案嵌入容量不足，熵利用率低，无法适配大语言模型固有的低熵特性，导致可行嵌入容量严重受限。

Method: 基于列表解码策略：维护一个包含正确秘密信息的候选集合而非直接搜索正确消息，充分挖掘生成文本的信息内容；引入后缀匹配机制从候选中区分正确秘密信息；提供安全性和正确性的理论证明及理论容量下界推导。

Result: 在三个LLM和七个PSS基线上的实验表明，该方法在保持与先前方案相当的计算效率的同时，显著提升了嵌入容量。

Conclusion: 提出的列表解码式隐写方案有效解决了LLM低熵环境下嵌入容量受限的难题，实现了高容量与可证明安全的统一，且即插即用的特性便于实际部署。

Abstract: Steganography embeds secret messages in seemingly innocuous carriers for covert communication under surveillance. Current Provably Secure Steganography (PSS) schemes based on language models can guarantee computational indistinguishability between the covertext and stegotext. However, achieving high embedding capacity remains a challenge for existing PSS. The inefficient entropy utilization renders them not well-suited for Large Language Models (LLMs), whose inherent low-entropy tendencies severely constrain feasible embedding capacity. To address this, we propose a provably secure steganography scheme with a theoretically proved high capacity. Our scheme is based on the concept of list decoding: it maintains a set of candidates that contain the correct secret message, instead of directly finding the correct message with more effort. This strategy fully utilizes the information content of the generated text, yielding higher capacity. To ensure the correctness of our scheme, we further introduce a suffix-matching mechanism to distinguish the correct secret message from the candidates. We provide theoretical proofs for both the security and correctness of our scheme, alongside a derivation of its theoretical capacity lower bound.
  Our approach is plug-and-play, requiring only a direct replacement of the model's standard random sampling module. Experiments on three LLMs and seven PSS baselines demonstrate that our method achieves computational efficiency comparable to prior PSS schemes while delivering a substantial improvement in embedding capacity.

</details>


### [119] [CSC: Turning the Adversary's Poison against Itself](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21416)
*Yuchen Shi,Xin Guo,Huajie Chen,Tianqing Zhu,Bo Liu,Wanlei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Cluster Segregation Concealment (CSC)方法，通过早期特征聚类识别中毒样本并将其重标记为虚拟类别，在四个基准数据集上针对十二种投毒攻击实现接近零的攻击成功率，同时保持模型干净准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于毒物抑制的防御方法存在检测不足、模型效用下降等问题，亟需一种既能有效识别后门样本又不损害模型性能的防御机制。

Method: CSC包含两个阶段：(1) 隔离阶段——在早期训练轮次提取特征，使用DBSCAN聚类，基于类别多样性和密度指标识别异常簇；(2) 隐藏阶段——将识别出的中毒样本重标记为虚拟类，通过交叉熵损失微调分类器，用良性虚拟关联替代后门关联。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上针对十二种投毒攻击的评估中，CSC在九种最先进防御方法中表现最优，将平均攻击成功率降至接近零，且干净准确率损失极小。

Conclusion: CSC通过揭示中毒样本在潜空间中早期形成孤立簇的规律，实现了鲁棒的后门模式识别和有效的隐藏机制，为可信人工智能的发展做出了贡献。

Abstract: Poisoning-based backdoor attacks pose significant threats to deep neural networks by embedding triggers in training data, causing models to misclassify triggered inputs as adversary-specified labels while maintaining performance on clean data. Existing poison restraint-based defenses often suffer from inadequate detection against specific attack variants and compromise model utility through unlearning methods that lead to accuracy degradation. This paper conducts a comprehensive analysis of backdoor attack dynamics during model training, revealing that poisoned samples form isolated clusters in latent space early on, with triggers acting as dominant features distinct from benign ones. Leveraging these insights, we propose Cluster Segregation Concealment (CSC), a novel poison suppression defense. CSC first trains a deep neural network via standard supervised learning while segregating poisoned samples through feature extraction from early epochs, DBSCAN clustering, and identification of anomalous clusters based on class diversity and density metrics. In the concealment stage, identified poisoned samples are relabeled to a virtual class, and the model's classifier is fine-tuned using cross-entropy loss to replace the backdoor association with a benign virtual linkage, preserving overall accuracy. CSC was evaluated on four benchmark datasets against twelve poisoning-based attacks, CSC outperforms nine state-of-the-art defenses by reducing average attack success rates to near zero with minimal clean accuracy loss. Contributions include robust backdoor patterns identification, an effective concealment mechanism, and superior empirical validation, advancing trustworthy artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [120] [Differentially Private De-identification of Dutch Clinical Notes: A Comparative Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21421)
*Michele Miranda,Xinlan Yan,Nishant Mishra,Rachel Murphy,Ameen Abu-Hanna,Sébastien Bratières,Iacer Calixto*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次比较了差分隐私(DP)、命名实体识别(NER)和大语言模型(LLM)在荷兰语临床文本去标识化中的表现，发现单独使用DP会严重降低效用，但与LLM预处理结合可显著改善隐私-效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保护临床叙述中的患者隐私对于在GDPR和HIPAA等法规下实现医疗数据的二次使用至关重要，人工去标识化虽为金标准但成本高、速度慢，需要兼具隐私保证和高效用的自动化方法。

Method: 对DP、NER和LLM三种方法进行单独评估，并探索NER或LLM预处理后接DP的混合策略，通过隐私泄露分析和外部评估（实体与关系分类）来衡量性能。

Result: 单独使用DP机制会严重降低文本效用，但与语言预处理（尤其是基于LLM的遮蔽）结合后，能显著改善隐私-效用的权衡。

Conclusion: 混合策略（LLM预处理+DP）是临床文本去标识化的有效方案，在隐私保护和数据效用之间取得了更好的平衡。

Abstract: Protecting patient privacy in clinical narratives is essential for enabling secondary use of healthcare data under regulations such as GDPR and HIPAA. While manual de-identification remains the gold standard, it is costly and slow, motivating the need for automated methods that combine privacy guarantees with high utility. Most automated text de-identification pipelines employed named entity recognition (NER) to identify protected entities for redaction. Although methods based on differential privacy (DP) provide formal privacy guarantees, more recently also large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for text de-identification in the clinical domain. In this work, we present the first comparative study of DP, NER, and LLMs for Dutch clinical text de-identification. We investigate these methods separately as well as hybrid strategies that apply NER or LLM preprocessing prior to DP, and assess performance in terms of privacy leakage and extrinsic evaluation (entity and relation classification). We show that DP mechanisms alone degrade utility substantially, but combining them with linguistic preprocessing, especially LLM-based redaction, significantly improves the privacy-utility trade-off.

</details>


### [121] [CrossCommitVuln-Bench: A Dataset of Multi-Commit Python Vulnerabilities Invisible to Per-Commit Static Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21917)
*Arunabh Majumdar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了CrossCommitVuln-Bench基准测试，包含15个跨多个提交引入的真实Python漏洞。实验发现，每提交检测率仅为13%，累积模式检测率仅27%，表明当前SAST工具在检测跨提交漏洞方面存在严重不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态应用安全测试（SAST）工具通常按单个提交进行扫描，但许多真实漏洞的条件是跨多个提交逐步引入的，每个单独提交看似无害，组合起来却构成严重漏洞。作者旨在系统性地揭示和量化这一检测盲区。

Method: (1) 从15个真实世界Python CVE中手工标注了每个CVE的贡献提交链及每个提交规避单提交分析的原因；(2) 使用Semgrep和Bandit在单提交模式和累积模式（完整代码库）下进行基线评估；(3) 计算每提交检测率（CCDR）和累积检测率。

Result: (1) 每提交检测率仅为13%，87%的漏洞链对单提交SAST不可见；(2) 两次单提交检测质量很低：一次检测到的是安全修复提交（开发者会忽略告警），另一次仅检测到次要的硬编码密钥问题，完全遗漏了主要漏洞（200+未保护的API端点）；(3) 累积模式检测率仅27%，确认了基于快照的SAST工具常遗漏跨提交引入的漏洞。

Conclusion: 现有SAST工具在检测跨提交逐步引入的漏洞方面严重不足，需要开发支持跨提交分析的新型检测方法。数据集、标注方案和评估脚本已开源以支持相关研究。

Abstract: We present CrossCommitVuln-Bench, a curated benchmark of 15 real-world Python vulnerabilities (CVEs) in which the exploitable condition was introduced across multiple commits - each individually benign to per-commit static analysis - but collectively critical. We manually annotate each CVE with its contributing commit chain, a structured rationale for why each commit evades per-commit analysis, and baseline evaluations using Semgrep and Bandit in both per-commit and cumulative scanning modes. Our central finding: the per-commit detection rate (CCDR) is 13% across all 15 vulnerabilities - 87% of chains are invisible to per-commit SAST. Critically, both per-commit detections are qualitatively poor: one occurs on commits framed as security fixes (where developers suppress the alert), and the other detects only the minor hardcoded-key component while completely missing the primary vulnerability (200+ unprotected API endpoints). Even in cumulative mode (full codebase present), the detection rate is only 27%, confirming that snapshot-based SAST tools often miss vulnerabilities whose introduction spans multiple commits. The dataset, annotation schema, evaluation scripts, and reproducible baselines are released under open-source licenses to support research on cross-commit vulnerability detection.

</details>


### [122] [A Stackelberg Model for Hybridization in Cryptography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21436)
*Willie Kouam,Stefan Rass,Zahra Seyedi,Shahzad Ahmad,Eckhard Pfluegel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文将密码学中的安全博弈建模为Stackelberg博弈：防御者随机选择加密算法（经典、后量子或混合），攻击者观察到选择后优化其密码分析方法。防御者问题转化为线性规划，攻击者子问题通过动态规划求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用博弈论框架分析密码学中的安全互动，特别是防御者与攻击者之间的策略对抗，以在安全性与运营成本之间取得平衡。

Method: 将防御者与攻击者的交互建模为资源约束下的Stackelberg密码混合博弈；防御者随机化加密算法选择，攻击者观察后优化密码分析方法；攻击者子问题通过动态规划求解，防御者的Stackelberg优化转化为线性规划。

Result: 提出了攻击者子问题的动态规划求解方法，以及防御者Stackelberg优化的线性规划形式化。

Conclusion: 该博弈模型为密码混合策略的选择提供了理论框架，能够在资源约束下系统地平衡安全性与成本。

Abstract: Similar to a strategic interaction between rational and intelligent agents, cryptography problems can be examined through the prism of game theory. In this setting, the agent aiming to protect a message is called the defender, while the one attempting to decrypt it, generally for malicious purposes, is the attacker. To strengthen security in cryptography, various strategies have been developed, among which hybridization stands out as a key concept in modern cryptographic design. This strategy allows the defender to select among different encryption algorithms (classical, post-quantum, or hybrid) while carefully balancing security and operational costs. On the other side, the attacker, limited by available resources, chooses cryptanalysis methods capable of breaching the selected algorithm. We model this interaction as a Stackelberg cryptographic hybridization problem under resource constraints. Here, the defender randomizes over encryption algorithms, and the attacker observes the choice before selecting suitable cryptanalysis methods. The attacker's decision is framed as a conditional optimization problem, which we refer to as the ``attacker subgame''. We then propose a dynamic programming approach for the attacker's subgame, while the defender's Stackelberg optimization is formulated as a linear program.

</details>


### [123] [MCP Pitfall Lab: Exposing Developer Pitfalls in MCP Tool Server Security under Multi-Vector Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21477)
*Run Hao,Zhuoran Tan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MCP Pitfall Lab，一个针对MCP协议感知的安全测试框架，通过可复现的开发者陷阱场景、追踪驱动的验证机制，在邮件、文档、加密三个工作流中评估六种服务端变体与三类攻击家族，实验表明加固可将风险评分从10.0降至0.0，平均仅需27行代码，并发现63.2%的智能体叙事与追踪证据不符，强调了基于追踪的审计与回归测试的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP协议的多层设计及第三方服务端生态带来了工具元数据、不可信输出、跨工具流、多模态输入、供应链等多方面安全风险，现有基准测试主要衡量对恶意输入的鲁棒性，但缺乏可操作的修复指导。

Method: 设计了协议感知的安全测试框架MCP Pitfall Lab，将开发者陷阱形式化为可复现场景，通过MCP追踪和客观验证器（而非智能体自述）验证结果。实例化三个工作流挑战（邮件、文档、加密），含六种服务端变体（基线版与加固版），建模三类攻击：工具元数据投毒、傀儡服务端、多模态图像到工具链。

Result: Tier-1静态分析在四个静态可检查陷阱类别上F1=1.0，跨工具转发和图像到工具泄露需追踪依赖。加固消除所有Tier-1发现（29降至0），风险评分从10.0降至0.0，平均仅需27行代码修改。初步19次运行的邮件系统挑战中，63.2%的运行和100%的接收端操作运行中智能体叙事与追踪证据不一致。

Conclusion: MCP Pitfall Lab能够在现实多向量条件下对MCP工具服务端进行实用的端到端评估与加固，并揭示了基于追踪的审计和回归测试的重要性。

Abstract: Model Context Protocol (MCP) is increasingly adopted for tool-integrated LLM agents, but its multi-layer design and third-party server ecosystem expand risks across tool metadata, untrusted outputs, cross-tool flows, multimodal inputs, and supply-chain vectors. Existing MCP benchmarks largely measure robustness to malicious inputs but offer limited remediation guidance. We present MCP Pitfall Lab, a protocol-aware security testing framework that operationalizes developer pitfalls as reproducible scenarios and validates outcomes with MCP traces and objective validators (rather than agent self-report). We instantiate three workflow challenges (email, document, crypto) with six server variants (baseline and hardened) and model three attack families: tool-metadata poisoning, puppet servers, and multimodal image-to-tool chains, in a unified, trace-grounded evaluation. In Tier-1 static analysis over six variants (36 binary labels), our analyzer achieves F1 = 1.0 on four statically checkable pitfall classes (P1, P2, P5, P6) and flags cross-tool forwarding and image-to-tool leakage (P3, P4) as trace/dataflow-dependent. Applying recommended hardening eliminates all Tier-1 findings (29 to 0) and reduces the framework risk score (10.0 to 0.0) at a mean cost of 27 lines of code (LOC). Finally, in a preliminary 19-run corpus from the email system challenge (tool poisoning and puppet attacks), agent narratives diverge from trace evidence in 63.2% of runs and 100% of sink-action runs, motivating trace-based auditing and regression testing. Overall, Pitfall Lab enables practical, end-to-end assessment and hardening of MCP tool servers under realistic multi-vector conditions.

</details>


### [124] [Benchmarking the Utility of Privacy-Preserving Cox Regression Under Data-Driven Clipping Bounds: A Multi-Dataset Simulation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21491)
*Keita Fukuyama,Yukiko Mori,Tomohiro Kuroda,Hiroaki Kikuchi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了差分隐私（DP）机制（拉普拉斯机制和随机响应）在Cox比例风险模型上的统计效用影响，使用5个临床数据集、15个$\varepsilon$级别和1000次蒙特卡洛迭代。数据驱动的裁剪边界基于观察到的最小/最大值，不提供正式的$\varepsilon$-DP保证，结果代表正式DP下效用下降的乐观下限。研究发现：在标准DP水平（$\varepsilon \leq 1$）下，约90%的显著协变量失去显著性，预测性能接近随机水平；输入扰动中仅扰动协变量最佳，输出扰动在$\varepsilon \geq 5$时保持接近基线性能；实际应用中，$\varepsilon \geq 10$（预测性能）到$\varepsilon \geq 30$–60（显著性保持）是必要的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私虽然能保证个体隐私，但其对生存分析中统计效用的系统性影响评估仍然有限，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 使用5个临床数据集（n=168–6,524），15个$\varepsilon$级别（0.1–1000），1000次蒙特卡洛迭代。比较三种输入扰动（仅协变量、全部输入、离散时间模型）和输出扰动（基于dfbeta的敏感性），使用LRS、C-index和系数偏差作为评估指标。

Result: $\varepsilon \leq 1$时约90%显著协变量失去显著性，预测性能接近随机。仅扰动协变量效果最佳。$\varepsilon \geq 5$时输出扰动接近基线。实际需要$\varepsilon \geq 10$（预测）到$\varepsilon \geq 30$–60（显著性）。中等$\varepsilon$下假阳性率增加。

Conclusion: 差分隐私在生存分析中会导致显著的统计效用损失，特别是在标准隐私预算下。实际应用中需要较高的$\varepsilon$值才能维持可接受的模型性能，且需警惕中等$\varepsilon$下的假阳性问题。

Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) is a mathematical framework that guarantees individual privacy; however, systematic evaluation of its impact on statistical utility in survival analyses remains limited. In this study, we systematically evaluated the impact of DP mechanisms (Laplace mechanism and Randomized Response) with data-driven clipping bounds on the Cox proportional hazards model, using 5 clinical datasets ($n = 168$--$6{,}524$), 15 levels of $\varepsilon$ (0.1--1000), and $B = 1{,}000$ Monte Carlo iterations. The data-driven clipping bounds used here are observed min/max and therefore do not provide formal $\varepsilon$-DP guarantees; the results represent an optimistic lower bound on utility degradation under formal DP. We compared three types of input perturbations (covariates only, all inputs, and the discrete-time model) with output perturbations (dfbeta-based sensitivity), using loss of significance rate (LSR), C-index, and coefficient bias as metrics. At standard DP levels ($\varepsilon \leq 1$), approximately 90% (90--94%) of the significant covariates lost significance, even in the largest dataset ($n = 6{,}524$), and the predictive performance approached random levels (test C-index $\approx 0.5$) under many conditions. Among the input perturbation approaches, perturbing only covariates preserved the risk-set structure and achieved the best recovery, whereas output perturbation (dfbeta-based sensitivity) maintained near-baseline performance at $\varepsilon \geq 5$. At $n \approx 3{,}000$, the significance recovered rapidly at $\varepsilon = 3$--10; however, in practice, $\varepsilon \geq 10$ (for predictive performance) to $\varepsilon \geq 30$--60 (for significance preservation) is required. In the moderate-to-high $\varepsilon$ range, false-positive rates increased for variables whose baseline $p$-values were near the significance threshold.

</details>


### [125] [Stealthy Backdoor Attacks against LLMs Based on Natural Style Triggers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21700)
*Jiali Wei,Ming Fan,Guoheng Sun,Xicheng Zhang,Haijun Wang,Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BadStyle是一个完整的后门攻击框架，利用LLM生成带有隐式风格级触发器的自然、隐蔽的毒化样本，通过辅助目标损失稳定有效载荷注入，在多种LLM上实现高攻击成功率并保持强隐蔽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM后门攻击方法存在三个关键缺陷：触发模式显式不自然、长文本生成中攻击者指定载荷注入不可靠、威胁模型不完整导致后门投递和激活方式不明确。

Method: 使用LLM作为毒化样本生成器，构造带有隐式风格级触发器（保持语义和流畅性）的样本；设计辅助目标损失函数，强化毒化输入中目标内容的出现并惩罚其在良性响应中出现；在提示驱动和PEFT两种注入策略下进行系统评估。

Result: 在7种受害LLM（包括LLaMA、Phi、DeepSeek、GPT系列）上实现高攻击成功率；辅助目标损失使风格级触发器的ASR平均提升约30%；后门在未知下游部署场景中依然有效；能够持续规避输入级防御并通过简单伪装绕过输出级防御。

Conclusion: BadStyle通过风格级触发器和辅助目标损失解决了现有方法在自然性、稳定性和威胁模型完整性方面的不足，是一种实用且隐蔽的LLM后门攻击框架。

Abstract: The growing application of large language models (LLMs) in safety-critical domains has raised urgent concerns about their security. Many recent studies have demonstrated the feasibility of backdoor attacks against LLMs. However, existing methods suffer from three key shortcomings: explicit trigger patterns that compromise naturalness, unreliable injection of attacker-specified payloads in long-form generation, and incompletely specified threat models that obscure how backdoors are delivered and activated in practice. To address these gaps, we present BadStyle, a complete backdoor attack framework and pipeline. BadStyle leverages an LLM as a poisoned sample generator to construct natural and stealthy poisoned samples that carry imperceptible style-level triggers while preserving semantics and fluency. To stabilize payload injection during fine-tuning, we design an auxiliary target loss that reinforces the attacker-specified target content in responses to poisoned inputs and penalizes its emergence in benign responses. We further ground the attack in a realistic threat model and systematically evaluate BadStyle under both prompt-induced and PEFT-based injection strategies. Extensive experiments across seven victim LLMs, including LLaMA, Phi, DeepSeek, and GPT series, demonstrate that BadStyle achieves high attack success rates (ASRs) while maintaining strong stealthiness. The proposed auxiliary target loss substantially improves the stability of backdoor activation, yielding an average ASR improvement of around 30% across style-level triggers. Even in downstream deployment scenarios unknown during injection, the implanted backdoor remains effective. Moreover, BadStyle consistently evades representative input-level defenses and bypasses output-level defenses through simple camouflage.

</details>


### [126] [Adversarial Robustness of Near-Field Millimeter-Wave Imaging under Waveform-Domain Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21774)
*Lhamo Dorje,Jordan Madden,Soamar Homsi,Xiaohua Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了近场毫米波成像在波形域物理攻击下的对抗鲁棒性，提出了一种基于可微成像管道的差分成像攻击框架，实验表明毫米波成像算法高度脆弱，但深度学习算法比传统算法更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波成像已广泛应用于机场安检等安全关键场景，但其自身安全性尚未得到充分探索，现有研究缺乏对成像算法在物理攻击下鲁棒性的系统评估。

Method: 提出白盒对抗攻击模型，构建可微成像管道的差分成像攻击框架优化攻击波形；搭建毫米波成像测试平台采集真实波形数据，在10种代表性成像算法上进行实验评估。

Result: 毫米波成像算法对此类攻击高度脆弱，攻击者能以中等发射功率实现目标隐藏或篡改；值得注意的是，基于深度学习的成像算法比传统算法表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 揭示了毫米波成像系统存在严重安全风险，亟需开发更鲁棒和安全的成像系统。

Abstract: Near-field millimeter-wave (mmWave) imaging is widely deployed in safety-critical applications such as airport passenger screening, yet its own security remains largely unexplored. This paper presents a systematic study of the adversarial robustness of mmWave imaging algorithms under waveform-domain physical attacks that directly manipulate the image reconstruction process. We propose a practical white-box adversarial model and develop a differential imaging attack framework that leverages the differentiable imaging pipeline to optimize attack waveforms. We also construct a real measured dataset of clean and attack waveforms using a mmWave imaging testbed. Experiments on 10 representative imaging algorithms show that mmWave imaging is highly vulnerable to such attacks, enabling an adversary to conceal or alter targets with moderate transmission power. Surprisingly, deep-learning-based imaging algorithms demonstrate higher robustness than classical algorithms. These findings expose critical security risks and motivate the development of robust and secure mmWave imaging systems.

</details>


### [127] [Black-Box Skill Stealing Attack from Proprietary LLM Agents: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21829)
*Zihan Wang,Rui Zhang,Yu Liu,Chi Liu,Qingchuan Zhao,Hongwei Li,Guowen Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对LLM智能体系统中的黑盒技能窃取攻击进行实证研究，发现仅需3次交互即可提取专有技能内容，并提出了跨三阶段的防御策略，但现有防护仍不足以应对可自动化重复攻击的威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体技能经济生态的兴起（免费市场已发布90368个技能，付费市场超2000个listing和10万美元创作者收入），技能作为封装可重用能力的核心载体，其知识产权保护面临新的攻击面——对手可通过与公共智能体交互来提取隐藏的专有技能内容，而这一版权风险在现有智能体生态系统中被严重忽视。

Method: 首先从已有提示窃取方法推导出攻击分类法，然后构建自动化窃取提示生成智能体：从模型生成的种子提示出发，通过情景合理化（scenario rationalization）和结构注入（structure injection）进行扩展，并利用嵌入过滤（embedding filtering）确保多样性。在3种商业智能体架构和5种LLM上评估攻击效果，并设计涵盖输入、推理、输出三阶段的防御方案。

Result: 仅需3次交互即可成功提取智能体技能，构成严重的版权风险。所提出的三阶段防御措施虽取得较好效果，但由于攻击成本低且可自动化，对手可通过多次尝试不同变体来绕过防御（只需一次成功即可攻破受保护技能）。

Conclusion: 当前专有智能体生态系统严重忽视了技能版权风险，亟需更鲁棒的防御策略来提供更强的保护保障。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on skills to encapsulate reusable capabilities via progressively disclosed instructions. High-quality skills inject expert knowledge into general-purpose models, improving performance on specialized tasks. This quality and ease of dissemination drive the emergence of a skill economy: free skill marketplaces already report 90368 published skills, while paid marketplaces report more than 2000 listings and over $100,000 in creator earnings. Yet this growing marketplace also creates a new attack surface, as adversaries can interact with public agent to extract hidden proprietary skill content. We present the first empirical study of black-box skill stealing against LLM agent systems. To study this threat, we first derive an attack taxonomy from prior prompt-stealing methods and build an automated stealing prompt generation agent. This agent starts from model-generated seed prompts, expands them through scenario rationalization and structure injection, and enforces diversity via embedding filtering. This process yields a reproducible pipeline for evaluating agent systems. We evaluate such attacks across 3 commercial agent architectures and 5 LLMs. Our results show that agent skills can be extracted with only 3 interactions, posing a serious copyright risk. To mitigate this threat, we design defenses across three stages of the agent pipeline: input, inference, and output. Although these defenses achieve strong results, the attack remains inexpensive and readily automatable, allowing an adversary to launch repeated attempts with different variants; only one successful attempt is sufficient to compromise the protected skill. Overall, our findings suggest that these copyright risks are largely overlooked across proprietary agent ecosystems. We therefore advocate for more robust defense strategies that provide stronger protection guarantees.

</details>


### [128] [Cross-Modal Phantom: Coordinated Camera-LiDAR Spoofing Against Multi-Sensor Fusion in Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21841)
*Shahriar Rahman Khan,Raiful Hasan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了自动驾驶多传感器融合（MSF）中一种未被充分探索的漏洞：攻击者通过模拟红外投影和LiDAR信号注入的协同物理欺骗，使相机和LiDAR对同一虚假目标达成跨模态一致性，从而绕过MSF的冗余安全机制。在KITTI数据集上的实验表明，这种协同攻击对最先进的感知模型达到了85.5%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多传感器融合（MSF）被设计用于通过冗余性抵御单传感器故障，但融合过程本身可能引入新的且未被充分探索的安全漏洞。作者旨在探究攻击者能否通过制造跨传感器的一致性（使多个传感器对同一虚假目标达成一致）来绕过MSF的冗余保护机制。

Method: 设计了一种协调的、数据级（早期融合）攻击方法，模拟两个同步物理欺骗源的输出效果：红外（IR）投影诱导相机产生虚假检测，以及LiDAR信号注入产生匹配的3D点簇。由于未实际实施物理攻击硬件，攻击通过在传感器输出层面插入透视感知的图像块和三维空间对齐的合成LiDAR点簇来模拟物理欺骗的感知效果。

Result: 使用400个KITTI场景进行大规模评估，协调欺骗攻击对最先进的感知模型达到了85.5%的成功攻击率。

Conclusion: 恶意跨模态一致性可以破坏基于MSF的感知系统，揭示了现代自动驾驶汽车核心数据融合逻辑中的关键漏洞。这是首次为这种攻击方式提供定量证据。

Abstract: Autonomous Vehicles (AVs) increasingly depend on Multi-Sensor Fusion (MSF) to combine complementary modalities such as cameras and LiDAR for robust perception. While this redundancy is intended to safeguard against single-sensor failures, the fusion process itself introduces a subtle and underexplored vulnerability. In this work, we investigate whether an attacker can bypass MSF's redundancy by fabricating cross-sensor consistency, making multiple sensors agree on the same false object. We design a coordinated, data-level (early-fusion) attack that emulates the outcome of two synchronized physical spoofing sources: an infrared (IR) projection that induces a false camera detection and a LiDAR signal injection that produces a matching 3D point cluster. Rather than implementing the physical attack hardware, we simulate its sensor-level outcomes by inserting perspective-aware image patches and synthetic LiDAR point clusters aligned in 3D space. This approach preserves the perceptual effects that real IR and IEMI-based spoofing would create at the sensor output. Using 400 KITTI scenes, our large-scale evaluation shows that the coordinated spoofing deceives a state-of-the-art perception model with an 85.5% successful attack rate. These findings provide the first quantitative evidence that malicious cross-modal consistency can compromise MSF-based perception, revealing a critical vulnerability in the core data-fusion logic of modern autonomous vehicle systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [Agentic AI-Enabled Framework for Thermal Comfort and Building Energy Assessment in Tropical Urban Neighborhoods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21787)
*Po-Yen Lai,Xinyu Yang,Derrick Low,Huizhe Liu,Jian Cheng Wong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种融合大语言模型与轻量物理模型的智能体AI推理框架，用于评估新加坡城市热岛效应和建筑能耗，通过绿墙、冷涂层等策略改善热舒适并降低能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应对新加坡城市热岛效应和建筑能耗需求，需要一种能够自主推理并定量评估城市微气候与建筑能耗的跨学科工具。

Method: 构建智能体AI框架，LLM通过提示定制理解城市设计任务、提取相关政策并激活轻量物理模型，形成闭环推理-行动流程；轻量物理模型基于核心热力学和气流原理，简化传统模型以快速预测微气候变量（如建筑表面温度、地面辐射热、气流条件），进而估算热舒适指标（如PET）和建筑能耗。

Result: 该框架支持用户探索多种气候适应性建筑表面策略（如绿墙、冷涂层），可改善热舒适度并减少墙体热增益和能源需求。

Conclusion: LLM的自主推理能力与轻量物理模型的快速定量评估相结合，在可持续城市设计、室内外环境一体化及气候适应规划等跨学科领域展现出应用潜力。

Abstract: In response to the urban heat island effects and building energy demands in Singapore, this study proposes an agentic AI-enabled reasoning framework that integrates large language models (LLMs) with lightweight physics-based models. Through prompt customization, the LLMs interpret urban design tasks, extract relevant policies, and activate appropriate physics-based models for evaluation, forming a closed-loop reasoning-action process. These lightweight physics-based models leverage core thermal and airflow principles, streamlining conventional models to reduce computational time while predicting microclimate variables, such as building surface temperature, ground radiant heat, and airflow conditions, thereby enabling the estimation of thermal comfort indices, e.g., physiological equivalent temperature (PET), and building energy usage. This framework allows users to explore a variety of climate-resilient building surface strategies, e.g., green façades and cool paint applications, that improve thermal comfort while reducing wall heat gain and energy demand. By combining the autonomous reasoning capacity of LLMs with the rapid quantitative evaluation of lightweight physics-based models, the proposed system demonstrates potential for cross-disciplinary applications in sustainable urban design, indoor-outdoor environmental integration, and climate adaptation planning. The source code and data used in this study are available at: https://github.com/PgUpDn/urban-cooling-agent.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [130] [StarLoc: Pinpointing Transmitting LEO Satellites from a Single Passive Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21147)
*Ishani Janveja,Jida Zhang,Emerson Sie,Deepak Vasisht*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出StarLoc系统，利用轨道动力学约束和新型干涉式3D到达角估计技术，仅用三个天线即可实现低轨卫星的3D定位。在81颗Starlink卫星上的实验表明，3D角度估计误差小于0.7度，轨道范围误差小于5公里。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低轨卫星的3D定位是频谱管理、轨道确定和太空GPS备份等应用中的关键问题，现有方法需要大量天线或复杂设备，成本高昂。

Method: 利用轨道动力学约束（卫星运动位于3D空间中的2D流形），结合新型干涉式3D到达角估计技术，仅需2D平面上的三个天线即可完成3D定位与跟踪。

Result: 在81颗Starlink卫星的实测数据上，3D角度估计精度达到0.7度以内，轨道范围估计误差在5公里以内。

Conclusion: StarLoc有效降低了低轨卫星3D定位的天线数量需求，验证了轨道动力学约束在实际系统中的应用可行性，具有实用价值。

Abstract: This paper focuses on 3D localization of transmitting satellites in low Earth orbits (LEO). 3D localization of transmitters in low orbits is an important emerging problem for many applications such as spectrum management, orbit determination, and backup for GPS failures in orbit. We present StarLoc -- a system to geolocate transmitters in space using a combination of orbital modeling and a new interferometric 3D angle-of-arrival estimation technique. StarLoc's design relies on a unique insight -- the motion of satellites is governed by orbital dynamics and is therefore along a 2D manifold in a 3D space. This reduces the degrees of freedom in satellite motion and allows us to 3D-locate and track a satellite with just three antennas in a 2D plane. We evaluate the system using signal transmissions from 81 Starlink satellites. Our results show that StarLoc can estimate the 3D-angle of a satellite within 0.7 degrees and the orbital range within 5 km. Our dataset and implementation are available at: https://connectedsystemslab.github.io/starloc.

</details>


### [131] [SparKV: Overhead-Aware KV Cache Loading for Efficient On-Device LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21231)
*Hongyao Liu,Liuqun Zhai,Junyi Wang,Zhengru Fang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: SparKV 是一种结合云端 KV 流式传输与本地计算的适应性 KV 加载框架，通过智能决策每个 KV 块是流式传输还是本地计算，并重叠执行路径以减少延迟，同时支持运行时动态调整以应对无线连接和边缘资源波动，在多种数据集、LLM 和边缘设备上将首 token 延迟降低 1.3x-5.1x，能耗降低 1.5x-3.3x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备上的大语言模型推理面临硬件资源有限和 prefill 阶段（构建 KV 缓存）成本高昂的挑战，现有方法难以兼顾延迟、能耗和响应质量。

Method: SparKV 提出自适应 KV 加载框架：1) 对每个 KV 块进行成本建模，决策流式传输或本地计算；2) 重叠两种执行路径以隐藏延迟；3) 在运行时根据无线连接和边缘资源波动离线调度进行动态重平衡通信与计算成本。

Result: 在多种数据集、LLM 和边缘设备上，SparKV 将首 token 延迟降低 1.3x-5.1x，响应质量影响可忽略，每请求能耗降低 1.5x-3.3x。

Conclusion: SparKV 通过云边协同的适应性 KV 调度策略，有效解决了边缘设备上 LLM 推理的高延迟和高能耗问题，具备鲁棒性和实际部署价值。

Abstract: Efficient inference for on-device Large Language Models (LLMs) remains challenging due to limited hardware resources and the high cost of the prefill stage, which processes the full input context to construct Key-Value (KV) caches. We present SparKV, an adaptive KV loading framework that combines cloud-based KV streaming with on-device computation. SparKV models the cost of individual KV chunks and decides whether each chunk should be streamed or computed locally, while overlapping the two execution paths to reduce latency. To handle fluctuations in wireless connectivity and edge resource availability, SparKV further refines offline-generated schedules at runtime to rebalance communication and computation costs. Experiments across diverse datasets, LLMs, and edge devices show that SparKV reduces Time-to-First-Token by 1.3$x-5.1x with negligible impact on response quality, while lowering per-request energy consumption by 1.5x to 3.3x, demonstrating its robustness and practicality for real-world on-device deployment.

</details>


### [132] [An Efficient Wireless iBCI Headstage with Adaptive ADC Sample Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21247)
*Hongyao Liu,Junyi Wang,Liuqun Zhai*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了一种采用自适应ADC采样和尖峰检测的无线iBCI头戴系统，通过服务器驱动架构在采集层实现数据压缩，相比传统方法降低功耗达40mW，FPGA资源利用率减少3.2倍，同时保持运动与视觉任务的解码精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线iBCI在功耗和数据吞吐量方面面临严峻限制，传统应用层压缩方法计算密集且效率有限，亟需在更底层的采集环节实现数据量缩减。

Method: 采用服务器驱动架构，服务器学习最优的电极特定采样率向量，动态重新配置ADC硬件，在模数转换和放大环节直接减少数据量，而非依赖后数字化的计算密集型压缩处理。

Result: 在多个受试者和阵列上的实验表明，功耗降低高达40mW，FPGA资源利用率减少3.2倍，且在运动和视觉任务中解码精度保持或超过原有水平。

Conclusion: 该设计为长期在体记录提供了高度实用的解决方案，在功耗和资源效率上具有显著优势，且开源了原型系统。

Abstract: Implantable Brain-Computer Interfaces (iBCIs) are increasingly pivotal in clinical and daily applications. However, wireless iBCIs face severe constraints in power consumption and data throughput. To mitigate these bottlenecks, we propose a wireless iBCI headstage featuring adaptive ADC sampling and spike detection. Distinguishing our design from traditional application-layer compression, we employ a server-driven architecture that achieves source-level efficiency. Specifically, the server learns an optimal, electrode-specific sample rate vector to dynamically reconfigure the ADC hardware. This strategy reduces data volume directly at the acquisition layer (ADC and amplifier) rather than relying on computationally intensive post-digitization processing. Extensive experiments across diverse subjects and arrays demonstrate a power reduction of up to 40 mW and a 3.2$\times$ decrease in FPGA resource utilization, all while maintaining or exceeding decoding accuracy in both motor and visual tasks. This design offers a highly practical solution for long-term in-vivo recording.Our prototype is open-sourced in: https://github.com/liuhongyao99cs/32-Channel-Wireless-BCI-Headstage.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [User-Centered Design of Hyperlocal Communication Platforms: Insights from the Design and Evaluation of KUBO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20973)
*Eljohn Evangelista,Alyssa Cea,Axel Balitaan,Clark Vince Diala,Jamlech Iram Gojo Cruz*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 针对菲律宾社区沟通中信息延迟、算法噪音、语言障碍和数字鸿沟四大问题，本文设计了KUBO双通道原型系统。与Facebook相比，KUBO显著缩短了任务完成时间、提高了信息回忆准确率和用户满意度，验证了面向超本地化场景的包容性平台能有效增强实时信息获取与公民参与。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 菲律宾超本地化通信中，紧急通知和社区事件信息因延迟推送或算法过滤而无法有效触达居民，现有平台存在多方面障碍，亟需设计更适合本地需求的沟通工具。

Method: 通过情境调查和半结构化访谈识别四大障碍，基于洞见设计KUBO原型（官方模块+社区模块），采用被试内设计将其与Facebook进行对比实验评估。

Result: KUBO在任务完成时间（p<0.001）、信息回忆准确率（p=0.010）和用户满意度（易用性、整体满意度、感知有效性）上均显著优于Facebook。

Conclusion: 双通道、包容性平台设计能显著提升超本地化场景下的实时信息获取、理解能力和公民参与度。

Abstract: Effective hyperlocal communication is critical in the Philippines, where delayed or algorithm-filtered updates can leave residents uninformed about emergency advisories and community events. We conducted a user-centered study consisting of contextual inquiry and semi-structured interviews to identify four key barriers: delayed alerts, algorithm-driven noise, language gaps, and digital divides. Guided by these insights, we designed KUBO (Kumunidad at Balitang Opisyal), a prototype that integrates a home module for verified local government unit advisories and curated headlines, and a community module for resident-powered neighborhood reports and discussions. Using a within-subjects evaluation design, KUBO significantly reduced task completion times (p-value < 0.001), improved information recall on post-task quizzes (p-value = 0.010), and yielded higher user satisfaction ratings for ease of use, overall satisfaction, and perceived effectiveness compared to Facebook, the commonly used communication platform in the Philippines. These results demonstrate that a dual-channel, inclusive platform can substantially enhance real-time information access, comprehension, and civic engagement in hyperlocal settings.

</details>


### [134] [When Constraints Limit and Inspire: Characterizing Presentation Authoring Practices for Evolving Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21205)
*Linxiu Zeng,Emily Kuang,Jian Zhao*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出基于约束的多会话演示文稿创作框架（CMPA），将时间、受众和沟通意图视为关键约束，并通过原型工具ReSlide验证了约束可主动驱动叙事构建和内容复用，而非仅被视为限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将约束视为局限性，但对演示者如何主动推理和利用约束缺乏了解，因此需要揭示约束在创作决策中的实际作用机制。

Method: 通过10名演示者的形成性研究提炼CMPA框架；开发ReSlide原型；开展两项用户研究分别评估单会话行为和多会话工作流程。

Result: 与基线工具相比，ReSlide帮助演示者将约束视为主动设计驱动力；在多会话中演示者能随约束演变灵活复用和调整内容。

Conclusion: 约束应被视作创作中的主动设计要素，未来约束感知工具应支持跨会话的灵活内容复用与适应。

Abstract: Authoring presentation slides involves navigating contextual constraints that shape how content is structured, adapted, and reused. While prior work frames constraints as limitations, little is known about how presenters actively reason about them. We conducted a formative study with ten presenters to examine how constraints emerge, are interpreted, and influence authoring decisions, leading to the Constraint-based Multi-session Presentation Authoring (CMPA) framework. CMPA treats time, audience, and communicative intent as key constraints shaping authoring. We instantiated CMPA in ReSlide, a research prototype for constraint-aware slide creation and reuse, and conducted two user studies on (1) single-session behaviors and (2) multi-session workflows. Compared to a baseline tool, ReSlide helped presenters treat constraints as active design drivers that guide narrative construction. The second study further shows how presenters flexibly reuse and adapt content across authoring cycles as constraints evolve. We then propose design implications for future constraint-aware presentation tools.

</details>


### [135] [TopoStyle: Supporting Iterative Design with Generative AI for 2.5D Topology Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21315)
*Shuyue Feng,Cedric Caremel,Yoshihiro Kawahara*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出TopoStyle，一种基于2D扩散模型的2.5D拓扑优化迭代设计工具，通过手绘交互和3D软件点交互两种方式，支持掩码区域优化，在平衡结构性能与美学质量的同时提升设计效率与定制化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有2D拓扑优化设计工具结果多样性不足、可定制性弱；生成式AI虽解决了计算成本问题但交互性有限；传统TO缺乏对结构性能与美学质量平衡迭代设计的支持。

Method: 基于2D扩散模型实现2.5D拓扑优化；提供两种交互方式（图形界面手绘交互和3D建模软件点交互）；支持掩码指定区域进行拓扑优化。

Result: 通过性能与交互方法的对比评估，验证了TopoStyle在平衡性能与美学、提升设计效率方面的有效性，并通过多个设计案例展示了应用场景。

Conclusion: TopoStyle通过迭代设计与定制化交互，有效解决了传统TO在多样性、交互性和美学-性能平衡方面的不足，为2.5D拓扑优化提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Topology optimization(TO) is widely used in engineering because of its ability to save material and optimize structural performance. Although prior work has explored 2D human-centered design tool for TO, the results are often limited in variety and offer weak customizability. Meanwhile, due to the high computational and time costs of TO, researchers have attempted to address these issues using generative AI; however, such methods often provide limited interactivity. In addition, topology optimization in many cases needs to balance structural performance and aesthetic qualities through iterative design, a perspective that has rarely been emphasized in traditional TO. We present TopoStyle, an iterative design tool for 2.5D topology optimization using a 2D diffusion model. We explore two interaction methods. The first exports 3D parts to a graphical interface for hand-drawn interaction. The second enables direct interaction within 3D modeling software using points. Our tool also supports the use of masks to apply topology optimization to specific regions, allowing users to address customized design needs. We compare and evaluate both performance and interaction methods, and investigate how TopoStyle can balance performance and aesthetics while improving design efficiency through customization and iterative design. Finally, we demonstrate the application scenarios of TopoStyle through several design cases.

</details>


### [136] ["If We Had the Information That We Need to Interpret the World Around Us, We Wouldn't Be Disabled:" Barriers and Opportunities in Information Work among Blind and Sighted Colleagues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21338)
*Yichun Zhao,Miguel A. Nacenta,Mahadeo A. Sukhai,Sowmya Somanath*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过日记研究、访谈和焦点小组，调查了视觉能力不同的专业团队在工作场所中使用信息表征（如PDF、电子表格和图表）的情况，揭示了四种相互关联的失败类型及相应变通方法，这些失败和变通受职场污名影响，并由相互依赖信息工作演变的社交动态所塑造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管人们认识到多样性的价值，但现有的工作方式未能充分支持盲人或低视力员工，尤其是在协作工作环境中。本研究旨在理解混合视觉能力团队如何使用信息表征，以及其中存在的障碍和变通方法。

Method: 日记研究配合后续个人访谈（来自5个团队的23名混合能力参与者），以及2个独立的焦点小组（来自另外2个团队的7名参与者）。

Result: 表征在工作场所中的作用被归纳为四种相互关联的失败类型及其对应的变通方法，这些失败与变通受职场污名影响，并随着团队向相互依赖的信息工作演变而不断变化。

Conclusion: 本研究提出了一个基于实证的新概念理解，有助于在当前技术环境下设计和改善混合能力团队进行知识工作的体验。

Abstract: Despite recognition of the value of diversity, the way work takes place can fail to support blind or low-vision employees, especially in collaborative work settings. This paper examines how professional teams with diverse visual abilities use information representations (e.g., PDF documents, spreadsheets and charts). A diary study with follow-up individual interviews (23 participants with mixed abilities from 5 teams) and 2 separate focus groups (7 participants from 2 other teams) allowed us to characterize key dimensions of the role of representations in the workplace into four types of interrelated failures and workarounds, influenced by workplace stigmas and shaped by evolving social dynamics towards interdependent information work. We contribute this new empirically supported conceptual understanding of representation use in workplaces that can help design and improve the experiences of mixed-ability teams doing knowledge work in the current technological landscape.

</details>


### [137] [Neurodiversity and Technostress: Towards a Multimodal Research Design for Evaluating Subjective, Physiological, and Behavioral Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21404)
*Lisa van den Heuvel,Igor Ivkić,René Riedl*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个整合多模态测量（主观、生理、行为）的受控实验设计，系统比较神经典型与神经多样性群体在标准化数字压力条件下的技术压力反应，以推动更包容的数字工作设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技术压力研究主要关注神经典型人群，且很少在单一设计中整合多个压力维度（主观、生理、行为）。本研究旨在填补这一空白，通过将神经多样性纳入技术压力研究，实现对数字压力的更差异化理解。

Method: 提出一个受控实验研究设计，包含结构化和非结构化数字任务，结合多模态测量方法：主观感知、生理激活和可观察的交互行为，系统比较神经多样性与神经典型个体。

Result: 提供一个整合神经多样性的技术压力研究方法论框架，能够更全面地捕捉不同人群在标准化数字压力条件下的多维度应激反应。

Conclusion: 该设计有助于更差异化地理解数字压力，并为更具包容性的数字工作设计提供方法论基础。

Abstract: Digitalization has transformed modern work by increasing efficiency while also introducing new forms of strain. Technostress (TS) describes subjective, physiological, and behavioral stress responses related to digital technology use. Existing TS research has predominantly focused on neurotypical populations and rarely integrates multiple stress dimensions within a single design. This paper addresses these gaps by proposing a controlled experimental research design that systematically compares neurodivergent and neurotypical individuals under standardized digital stress conditions. The proposed design combines structured and unstructured digital tasks with a multimodal measurement approach covering subjective perceptions, physiological activation, and observable interaction behavior. By integrating neurodiversity into TS research, the paper contributes to a more differentiated understanding of digital stress and provides a methodological approach for more inclusive digital work design.

</details>


### [138] [The Privacy Guardian Agent: Towards Trustworthy AI Privacy Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21455)
*Vincent Freiberger*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 针对当前"通知与同意"模式存在的操纵性、阅读负担重等问题，本文提出一种折中方案——隐私守护智能体，通过用户画像与上下文感知实现常规同意的自动化处理，同时保留人工介入与结果可解释机制，旨在减轻同意疲劳的同时维护用户信任与自主性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的"通知与同意"范式已失效：同意对话框常具有操纵性，用户无法真实阅读每份隐私政策。基于LLM的工具虽赋能用户主动控制，但许多时间或动机有限的用户更偏好完全自动化方案，而后者存在幻觉与决策不透明问题，损害信任。

Method: 提出隐私守护智能体，利用用户画像与上下文感知自动化常规同意选择，同时识别不确定性；对不明确或高风险案例升级至用户处理（人在回路）；其自主决策的推理过程可被审查，确保可解释性与用户申诉权；对即使最小同意也存在问题的案例，向用户发出警报并建议切换至替代网站。

Result: 该方案能够在减少同意疲劳的同时维护用户信任与有意义的自主性。

Conclusion: 通过自动化与人工介入的平衡、决策过程的可审查性以及问题案例的警报机制，隐私守护智能体为当前有缺陷的同意范式提供了可行的改进路径。

Abstract: The current "notice and consent" paradigm is broken: consent dialogues are often manipulative, and users cannot realistically read or understand every privacy policy. While recent LLM-based tools empower users seeking active control, many with limited time or motivation prefer full automation. However, fully autonomous solutions risk hallucinations and opaque decisions, undermining trust. I propose a middle ground - a Privacy Guardian Agent that automates routine consent choices using user profiles and contextual awareness while recognizing uncertainty. It escalates unclear or high-risk cases to the user, maintaining a human-in-the-loop only when necessary. To ensure agency and transparency, the agent's reasoning on its autonomous decisions is reviewable, allowing for user recourse. For problematic cases, even with minimal consent, it alerts the user and suggests switching to an alternative site. This approach aims to reduce consent fatigue while preserving trust and meaningful user autonomy.

</details>


### [139] [Gradual Voluntary Participation: A Framework for Participatory AI Governance in Journalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21878)
*Matilde Barbini,Stefano Sorrentino,Daniel Gatica-Perez*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出渐进自愿参与（GVP）框架，重新定义AI新闻编辑室中的参与式设计，将参与视为渐进自愿的过程，通过二维矩阵模型平衡技术变革与利益相关者赋权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统参与式设计假设用户能影响技术，但AI系统因数据不透明、架构固定、目标不可达而难以被影响。新闻编辑室面临感知差距——对AI的信任取决于员工在工作流程中感知到的自主权，需要新的参与框架。

Method: 通过对10名新闻工作者的访谈，识别感知差距；基于发现提出GVP框架的五大核心原则，采用二维矩阵结构（深度×广度）替代传统阶梯隐喻。

Result: 揭示了信任与感知自主权之间的关联；构建了GVP框架，将渐进性与自愿性作为设计维度，解决了认识论负担、参与上限和表演性咨询等问题。

Conclusion: GVP框架为混合工作场所中本地化参与式AI治理提供了新模型，超越了固定工作坊或一次性偏好征集，平衡技术转型与利益相关者赋权。

Abstract: The integration of AI into journalism challenges participatory design (PD), particularly with respect to stakeholder influence, workplace perceptions, and organizational dynamics. Traditional PD assumes that users can shape technologies, yet AI systems resist influence due to opaque data, fixed architectures, and inaccessible objectives. Through interviews with 10 journalists, we identify the perception gap, showing that trust in AI depends on perceived agency within workplace participatory workflows. Informed by these findings, we introduce the Gradual Voluntary Participation (GVP) framework in journalism and its five core principles, reconceptualizing participation as a gradual and voluntary process that can be operationalized at the newsroom level, beyond fixed workshops or one-time preference-elicitation campaigns. Addressing epistemic burdens, participatory ceilings, and performative consultations, GVP treats gradualism and voluntariness as design dimensions that shape perception, legitimacy, and ownership. Moving beyond unidimensional ladder metaphors and adopting a bidimensional matrix structure, the framework maps stakeholders across depth and scope, offering a new model for local participatory AI governance that balances technological transformation with stakeholder empowerment in rapidly evolving hybrid workplaces.

</details>

# cs.OH [[Back]](#toc)

### [140] [Institutionalizing Best Practices in Research Computing: A Framework and Case Study for Improving User Onboarding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21898)
*Ayush Chaturvedi,Rob Pokorney,Elyn Fritz-Waters,Charlotte Rouse,Gary Bax,Daryl Spencer,Craig Pohl*

Main category: cs.OH

TL;DR: 本文针对科研计算中心新用户 onboarding 困难的问题，提出了一套改进 onboarding 体验的框架，并通过在圣路易斯华盛顿大学研究基础设施服务中的实际应用进行了实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科研计算中心的新用户在面对复杂的基础设施和软件时常常感到困惑和不知所措，尽管有文档、教程和培训等资源，用户仍然难以有效访问和使用这些资源，因此需要一套系统化的方法来改善新用户的 onboarding 体验。

Method: 提出了一套用于改善新用户 onboarding 体验的框架，并在圣路易斯华盛顿大学的研究基础设施服务中进行了实际应用和实证验证。

Result: 所提出的框架在圣路易斯华盛顿大学的研究基础设施服务中得到了实证验证。

Conclusion: 本文提出的框架能够有效改善科研计算中心新用户的 onboarding 体验。

Abstract: Research computing centers around the world struggle with onboarding new users. Subject matter experts, researchers, and principal investigators are often overwhelmed by the complex infrastructure and software offerings designed to support diverse research domains at large academic and national institutions. As a result, users frequently fall into confusion and complexity to access these resources, despite the availability of documentation, tutorials, interactive trainings and other similar resources. Through this work, we present a framework designed to improve new-user onboarding experience. We also present an empirical validation through its application within the Research Infrastructure Services at Washington University in St. Louis.

</details>
